How Vulnerable Is My Learned Policy? Universal Adversarial Perturbation Attacks On Modern Behavior Cloning Policies
Authors: Akansha Kalra, Basavasagar Patil, Guanhong Tao, Daniel S. Brown
First: 2025-02-06T01:17:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:18:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Learning from demonstrations is a popular approach to train AI models; however, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks remains underexplored. We present the first systematic study of adversarial attacks, across a range of both classic and recently proposed imitation learning algorithms, including Vanilla Behavior Cloning (Vanilla BC), LSTM-GMM, Implicit Behavior Cloning (IBC), Diffusion Policy (DP), and Vector-Quantized Behavior Transformer (VQ-BET). We study the vulnerability of these methods to both white-box, grey-box and black-box adversarial perturbations. Our experiments reveal that most existing methods are highly vulnerable to these attacks, including black-box transfer attacks that transfer across algorithms. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to study and compare the vulnerabilities of different popular imitation learning algorithms to both white-box and black-box attacks. Our findings highlight the vulnerabilities of modern imitation learning algorithms, paving the way for future work in addressing such limitations. Videos and code are available at https://sites.google.com/view/uap-attacks-on-bc.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess the robustness of imitation learning models against adversarial threats, this study systematically evaluates the vulnerability of modern behavior cloning policies to universal adversarial perturbations under white-box, grey-box, and black-box attack settings. It compares multiple algorithms including Vanilla BC, LSTM-GMM, IBC, DP, and VQ-BET, revealing that most are highly susceptible to such attacks, including cross-algorithm black-box transfer attacks. Experimental results demonstrate that adversarial perturbations can successfully deceive these models even without access to their internal parameters, underscoring a critical security gap in current imitation learning approaches.
为评估模仿学习的安全性，本研究系统评估了多种现代行为克隆算法（包括Vanilla BC、LSTM-GMM、IBC、DP和VQ-BET）在白盒、灰盒及黑盒对抗扰动下的脆弱性。实验表明，多数算法对攻击高度敏感，甚至黑盒攻击也能跨算法有效迁移并破坏性能，揭示了当前模仿学习方法中存在的关键安全缺陷。
Quality-Driven Selective Mutation for Deep Learning
Authors: Zaheed Ahmed, Emmanuel Charleson Dapaah, Philip Makedonski, Jens Grabowski
First: 2026-04-24T15:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:15:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mutants support testing and debugging in two roles: (i) as test goals and (ii) as substitutes for real faults. Hard-to-kill mutants provide better guidance for test improvement, while realism is essential when mutants are used to simulate real bugs. Building on these roles, selective mutation for deep learning (DL) aims to reduce the cost of mutant generation and execution by choosing operator configurations that yield resistant and realistic mutants. However, the DL literature lacks a unified measure that captures both aspects. This study presents a probabilistic framework to quantify mutant quality along two complementary axes: resistance and realism. Resistance adapts the classical notion of hard-to-kill mutants to the DL setting using statistical killing probabilities, while realism is measured via the generalized Jaccard similarity between mutant and real-fault detectability patterns. The framework enables ranking and filtering of low-quality mutation-operator configurations without assuming a specific use case. We empirically evaluate the approach on four datasets of real DL faults. Three datasets (CleanML, DeepFD, and DeepLocalize) are used to estimate and select high-quality operator configurations, and the held-out defect4ML dataset is used for validation. Results show that quality-driven selection reduces the number of generated mutants by up to 55.6% while preserving typical levels of resistance and realism under baseline-aligned selection thresholds. These findings confirm that dual-objective selection can lower cost without compromising the usefulness of mutants for either role.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向深度学习的质量驱动选择性变异
变异体在测试和调试中扮演两种角色：(i) 作为测试目标，(ii) 作为真实缺陷的替代品。难以杀死的变异体能更好地指导测试改进，而当变异体用于模拟真实缺陷时，现实性至关重要。基于这些角色，面向深度学习的选择性变异旨在通过选择生成抗性更强且更现实的变异体的算子配置，降低变异体生成与执行的成本。然而，深度学习文献中缺乏统一的度量标准来同时衡量这两个方面。本研究提出一个概率框架，从两个互补维度——抗性与现实性——量化变异体质量。抗性通过统计杀灭概率将经典“难以杀死”的概念适配到深度学习场景；现实性则通过变异体与真实缺陷检测模式之间的广义Jaccard相似度进行衡量。该框架无需假设特定应用场景，即可对低质量变异算子配置进行排序与过滤。我们在四个真实深度学习缺陷数据集上对方法进行实证评估：其中三个数据集（CleanML、DeepFD、DeepLocalize）用于估计并选择高质量算子配置，保留的缺陷4ML数据集用于验证。结果表明，在与基线对齐的筛选阈值下，质量驱动的选择可将生成的变异体数量减少高达55.6%，同时保持典型的抗性与现实性水平。这些发现证实，双目标选择可在不损害变异体在任一角色中效用的前提下降低成本。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the inefficiency of mutant generation in deep learning testing by proposing a quality-driven selective mutation framework that balances resistance and realism. Motivated by the need to reduce computational cost while maintaining mutant utility for test improvement and fault simulation, the method introduces a probabilistic model quantifying mutant quality along two axes: resistance (via statistical killing probabilities) and realism (via Jaccard similarity with real-fault detectability patterns). Evaluated across four real-world deep learning fault datasets, the approach successfully filters low-quality operator configurations, reducing mutant generation by up to 55.6% while preserving resistance and realism under baseline-aligned thresholds, demonstrating that dual-objective selection can significantly cut costs without sacrificing mutant effectiveness.
为降低深度学习中变异体生成成本同时保持其作为测试目标和真实缺陷模拟器的有效性，该研究提出一种概率框架，从抵抗性（基于统计杀灭概率）和真实性（基于与真实缺陷检测模式的Jaccard相似度）两个维度量化变异体质量。该方法无需假设特定应用场景即可对变异操作符配置进行排序和筛选，并在四个真实深度学习缺陷数据集上进行实验。结果表明，质量驱动的选择可将变异体生成量减少高达55.6%，同时在基线阈值下保持抵抗性和真实性的典型水平，证明双目标选择可在不牺牲变异体效用的前提下显著降低成本。
Adversarial Malware Generation in Linux ELF Binaries via Semantic-Preserving Transformations
Authors: Lukáš Hrdonka, Martin Jureček
First: 2026-04-24T15:14:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:14:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Malware development and detection have undergone significant changes in recent years as modern concepts, such as machine learning, have been used for both adversarial attacks and defense. Despite intensive research on Windows Portable Executable (PE) files, there is minimal work on Linux Executable and Linkable Format (ELF). In this work, we summarize the academic papers submitted in this field and develop a new adversarial malware generator for the ELF format. Using a variety of metrics, we thoroughly evaluated our generator and achieved an Evasion Rate of 67.74 % while changing the confidence of the malware detector by -0.50 in the mean case for the dataset used. In our approach, we chose MalConv as the target classifier. Using this classifier, we found that the most successful modifications used strings typical of benign files as a data source. We conducted a variety of experiments and concluded that the target classifier appears sensitive to strings at any location within the executable file.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过语义保持变换在Linux ELF二进制文件中生成对抗性恶意软件
近年来，随着机器学习等现代概念被用于对抗攻击和防御，恶意软件的开发与检测发生了显著变化。尽管对Windows可移植可执行（PE）文件已有大量研究，但针对Linux可执行与链接格式（ELF）的研究极少。本文综述了该领域的学术论文，并开发了一种新的针对ELF格式的对抗性恶意软件生成器。通过多种指标全面评估该生成器，在所用数据集上实现了67.74%的逃逸率，且平均降低了检测器对恶意软件的置信度0.50。在本方法中，我们选择MalConv作为目标分类器。使用该分类器发现，最成功的修改通常以良性文件中常见的字符串作为数据源。我们进行了多种实验，得出结论：目标分类器对可执行文件内任何位置的字符串均较为敏感。
Summary / 总结
Malware development and detection have undergone significant changes in recent years as modern concepts, such as machine learning, have been used for both adversarial attacks and defense.
鉴于当前针对Linux ELF二进制文件的对抗性恶意软件生成研究极少，尽管机器学习在恶意软件检测中日益普及，本研究开发了一种新的生成器，通过语义保持的变换来规避检测。该方法以MalConv分类器为目标，利用良性文件中的字符串对ELF二进制文件进行修改，同时保持其功能。实验结果显示，该生成器在测试数据集上实现了67.74%的逃逸率，平均降低检测器置信度0.50，表明该分类器对可插入到可执行文件任意位置的字符串尤为敏感。
CLVAE: A Variational Autoencoder for Long-Term Customer Revenue Forecasting
Authors: Jeffrey Näf, Riana Valera Mbelson, Markus Meierer
First: 2026-04-24T15:12:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:12:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting customers' long-term revenue from sparse and irregular transaction data is central to marketing resource allocation in non-contractual settings, yet existing approaches face a trade-off. Traditional probabilistic customer base models deliver robust long-horizon forecasts by imposing strong structural assumptions, while flexible machine-learning models often require substantial training data and careful tuning. We propose a variational-autoencoder-based model that preserves the process-based likelihood of established attrition-transaction-spend models conditional on customer heterogeneity, but replaces the restrictive parametric mixing distribution with a flexible latent representation learned by encoder-decoder networks. The resulting approach (i) provides a single model for customer attrition, transactions and spending, (ii) remains reliable when contextual covariates are unavailable, and (iii) flexibly incorporates rich covariates and nonlinear effects when they are available. This design balances structural stability with the flexibility needed to capture complex purchase dynamics. Across multiple real-world datasets and prediction horizons, the proposed model improves upon the latest benchmarks. Businesses benefit directly, as a better assessment of customers' future revenues improves the efficiency of campaign targeting. For research, this work provides guidance on how to embed domain-specific models into the variational autoencoder framework, enabling flexible representation learning while retaining an econometrically meaningful process structure.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of forecasting long-term customer revenue from sparse, irregular transaction data in non-contractual marketing settings, this paper introduces CLVAE, a variational autoencoder that combines process-based likelihoods from attrition-transaction-spend models with flexible latent representations learned via encoder-decoder networks. The method replaces rigid parametric mixing distributions with learned latent variables, enabling a unified model for attrition, transactions, and spending that remains reliable without contextual covariates and can flexibly incorporate them when available. Experimental results across multiple real-world datasets and horizons show CLVAE outperforms recent benchmarks, offering improved revenue forecasting and more efficient campaign targeting.
为解决非合同营销场景下从稀疏不规则交易数据中预测长期客户收入的难题，本文提出CLVAE模型，该模型融合了传统流失-交易-消费过程的似然结构，同时用编码器-解码器网络学习的灵活潜在表示替代了传统参数化混合分布。该模型统一处理客户流失、交易与消费预测，无需上下文协变量时仍保持稳定，有协变量时可灵活引入丰富特征与非线性效应。在多个真实数据集与预测周期上，CLVAE显著优于现有基准模型，提升客户收入预测精度，助力精准营销，并为如何将领域特定模型嵌入变分自编码器框架以兼顾经济意义与灵活表征学习提供了方法指导。
On Pareto Optimality for Parametric Choice Bandits
Authors: Jierui Zuo, Hanzhang Qin
First: 2025-01-31T16:42:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:11:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study online assortment optimization under stochastic choice when a decision maker simultaneously values cumulative revenue performance and the quality of post-hoc inference on revenue contrasts. We analyze a forced-exploration optimism-in-the-face-of-uncertainty (OFU) scheme that combines two regularized maximum-likelihood estimators: one based on all observations for sequential decision making, and one based only on exploration rounds for inference. Our general theory is developed under predictable score proxies and per-round action-dependent curvature domination. Under these conditions we establish a self-normalized concentration inequality, a likelihood-based ellipsoidal confidence-set theorem, and a regret bound for approximate optimistic actions that explicitly accounts for optimization error. For the multinomial logit (MNL) model we derive explicit score and curvature proxies and show that a balanced spaced singleton-exploration schedule yields realized coordinate coverage, implying regret $\Otilde(n_T + T/\sqrt{n_T})$ and revenue-contrast error $\Otilde(1/\sqrt{n_T})$ up to fixed problem-dependent factors. A hard two-assortment subclass yields a matching lower bound at the product level. Consequently, within the polynomial exploration family $n_T \asymp T^α$, the regret and inference rates become $\Otilde(T^{\max\{α,1-α/2\}})$ and $\Otilde(T^{-α/2})$, respectively; hence $α\in[2/3,1)$ is the rate-wise Pareto-undominated interval and $α=2/3$ is the unique balancing point that minimizes the regret exponent. Finally, for the Exponomial Choice and Nested Logit models we state verifiable sufficient conditions that would instantiate the general framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数选择带中的帕累托最优性研究
我们研究在随机选择下，决策者同时重视累积收益表现与事后收益对比推断质量的在线组合优化问题。我们分析了一种强制探索的乐观面对不确定性（OFU）方案，该方案结合了两个正则化最大似然估计器：一个基于所有观测用于顺序决策，另一个仅基于探索轮次用于推断。在可预测得分代理和每轮动作依赖曲率主导条件下，我们建立了自归一化集中不等式、基于似然的椭球置信集定理及考虑优化误差的近似乐观动作的遗憾界。对于多项逻辑（MNL）模型，我们推导出显式得分与曲率代理，证明平衡间隔单例探索方案可实现坐标覆盖，从而得到遗憾 $\Otilde(n_T + T/\sqrt{n_T})$ 与收益对比误差 $\Otilde(1/\sqrt{n_T})$（至固定问题相关因子）。硬性两组合子类在乘积层面匹配下界。因此，在多项式探索族 $n_T \asymp T^α$ 中，遗憾与推断速率分别为 $\Otilde(T^{\max\{α,1-α/2\}})$ 与 $\Otilde(T^{-α/2})$；故 $α\in[2/3,1)$ 为速率层面帕累托非支配区间，且 $α=2/3$ 为唯一最小化遗憾指数的平衡点。最后，对于指数多项选择与嵌套逻辑模型，我们陈述了可验证的充分条件以实例化该通用框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance revenue optimization with reliable post-hoc inference in online assortment selection, this work introduces an optimism-in-the-face-of-uncertainty (OFU) algorithm that uses two regularized maximum-likelihood estimators—one for sequential decisions and one for inference—under predictable score proxies and curvature constraints. Theoretical guarantees include self-normalized concentration, confidence-set bounds, and regret analysis that accounts for optimization error. For the multinomial logit model, a balanced singleton-exploration schedule achieves regret and inference error bounds scaling as $\Otilde(n_T + T/\sqrt{n_T})$ and $\Otilde(1/\sqrt{n_T})$, respectively, with a matching lower bound for a hard subclass. Within polynomial exploration $n_T \asymp T^α$, the regret and inference rates become $\Otilde(T^{\max\{α,1-α/2\}})$ and $\Otilde(T^{-α/2})$, revealing that $α\in[2/3,1)$ is Pareto-optimal and $α=2/3$ uniquely minimizes the regret exponent. The framework is also extended to Exponomial and Nested Logit models via verifiable conditions.
为在在线选品优化中同时兼顾累积收益与事后推断质量，本文提出一种基于乐观探索的算法，结合两个正则化最大似然估计器——一个用于决策，一个用于推断——在可预测得分代理和每轮动作依赖曲率支配条件下建立理论框架。该方法推导出自归一化集中不等式、似然椭球置信集定理，并给出包含优化误差的遗憾界。在多项式逻辑模型中，平衡的单元素探索策略实现坐标覆盖，遗憾与推断误差分别为 $\Otilde(n_T + T/\sqrt{n_T})$ 和 $\Otilde(1/\sqrt{n_T})$，且在两选品子类中达到匹配下界。在多项式探索 $n_T \asymp T^α$ 下，遗憾与推断率分别为 $\Otilde(T^{\max\{α,1-α/2\}})$ 和 $\Otilde(T^{-α/2})$，表明 $α\in[2/3,1)$ 为帕累托非支配区间，且 $α=2/3$ 唯一最小化遗憾指数。框架还可扩展至指数多项式与嵌套逻辑模型，通过可验证充分条件实现。
Survey Response Generation: Generating Closed-Ended Survey Responses In-Silico with Large Language Models
Authors: Georg Ahnert, Anna-Carolina Haensch, Barbara Plank, Markus Strohmaier
First: 2025-10-13T16:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:10:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many in-silico simulations of human survey responses with large language models (LLMs) focus on generating closed-ended survey responses, whereas LLMs are typically trained to generate open-ended text instead. Previous research has used a diverse range of methods for generating closed-ended survey responses with LLMs, and a standard practice remains to be identified. In this paper, we systematically investigate the impact that various Survey Response Generation Methods have on predicted survey responses. We present the results of 32 mio. simulated survey responses across 8 Survey Response Generation Methods, 4 political attitude surveys, and 10 open-weight language models. We find significant differences between the Survey Response Generation Methods in both individual-level and subpopulation-level alignment. Our results show that Restricted Generation Methods perform best overall, and that reasoning output does not consistently improve alignment. Our work underlines the significant impact that Survey Response Generation Methods have on simulated survey responses, and we develop practical recommendations on the application of Survey Response Generation Methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate realistic closed-ended survey responses using large language models (LLMs), which are typically trained for open-ended tasks, this study systematically evaluates 8 different generation methods across 4 political attitude surveys and 10 open-weight models, producing 32 million simulated responses. The authors find that restricted generation methods yield the most aligned responses at both individual and subpopulation levels, while reasoning-based approaches do not consistently improve alignment. Their results highlight the critical influence of generation method choice on simulated survey outcomes and offer practical guidance for selecting appropriate techniques.
为解决大语言模型（LLM）虽擅长生成开放式文本但常用于生成闭合式问卷回答的问题，本研究系统评估了8种生成方法在4个政治态度问卷和10个开源语言模型上的表现，共生成3200万条模拟回答。结果表明，受限生成方法在个体与子群体层面的对齐效果最佳，而基于推理的方法并未稳定提升对齐度。研究强调生成方法对模拟问卷结果的显著影响，并提出实用建议以指导未来模拟研究中方法的选择。
Adapting MLLMs for Nuanced Video Retrieval
Authors: Piyush Bagad, Andrew Zisserman
First: 2025-12-15T16:38:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:10:21+00:00
Comments: 38 Pages. Project page at http://bpiyush.github.io/tara-website
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Our objective is to build an embedding model that captures the nuanced relationship between a search query and candidate videos. We cover three aspects of nuanced retrieval: (i) temporal, (ii) negation, and (iii) multimodal. For temporal nuance, we consider chiral actions that need distinguishing between temporally opposite actions like "opening a door" vs. "closing a door". For negation, we consider queries with negators such as "not", "none" that allow user to specify what they do not want. For multimodal nuance, we consider the task of composed retrieval where the query comprises a video along with a text edit instruction. The goal is to develop a unified embedding model that handles such nuances effectively. To that end, we repurpose a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) trained to generate text into an embedding model. We fine-tune it with a contrastive loss on text alone with carefully sampled hard negatives that instill the desired nuances in the learned embedding space. Despite the text-only training, our method achieves state of the art performance on all benchmarks for nuanced video retrieval. We also analyze how this improvement is achieved, and show that text-only training reduces the modality gap between text and video embeddings leading to better organization of the embedding space.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture fine-grained relationships in video retrieval, this work develops a unified embedding model that handles temporal, negation, and multimodal nuances. It repurposes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) originally trained for text generation and fine-tunes it using a contrastive loss on text-only data with carefully selected hard negatives to encode the desired semantic distinctions. Despite training solely on text, the model achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple nuanced video retrieval benchmarks, with analysis showing that text-only training reduces the modality gap and improves embedding space organization.
为提升视频检索中语义关系的捕捉能力，本研究构建了一种统一嵌入模型，专门处理时序、否定与多模态等细微语义。方法将原本用于文本生成的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）改造为嵌入模型，并通过在纯文本数据上使用对比损失和精心采样的难负例进行微调，以编码细微语义。实验表明，尽管仅使用文本训练，该模型在多个视频检索基准上达到最先进性能，分析显示纯文本训练缩小了文本与视频嵌入间的模态差距，从而优化了嵌入空间的组织结构。
PL-MTEB: Polish Massive Text Embedding Benchmark
Authors: Rafał Poświata, Sławomir Dadas, Michał Perełkiewicz
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2024-05-16T14:33:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:07:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted for ACL 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this paper, we introduce the Polish Massive Text Embedding Benchmark (PL-MTEB), a comprehensive benchmark for text embeddings in the Polish language. PL-MTEB comprises 30 diverse NLP tasks across five categories: classification, clustering, pair classification, information retrieval, and semantic text similarity. Within the scope of this work, we added 12 new Polish-language tasks to MTEB based on existing datasets and prepared two new datasets used to create four clustering tasks. We evaluated 30 publicly available text embedding models, including Polish and multilingual models. We analyzed the results in detail for specific task types and model sizes. We made the prepared datasets, the source code for evaluation, and the obtained results available to the public at https://github.com/rafalposwiata/pl-mteb.
Summary / 总结
In this paper, we introduce the Polish Massive Text Embedding Benchmark (PL-MTEB), a comprehensive benchmark for text embeddings in the Polish language.

Mixed Membership sub-Gaussian Models
Authors: Huan Qing
First: 2026-04-24T15:07:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:07:32+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Gaussian mixture model is widely used in unsupervised learning, owing to its simplicity and interpretability. However, a fundamental limitation of the classical Gaussian mixture model is that it forces each observation to belong to exactly one component. In many practical applications, such as genetics, social network analysis, and text mining, an observation may naturally belong to multiple components or exhibit partial membership in several latent components. To overcome this limitation, we propose the mixed membership sub-Gaussian model, which extends the classical Gaussian mixture framework by allowing each observation to belong to multiple components. This model inherits the interpretability of the classical Gaussian mixture model while offering greater flexibility for capturing complex overlapping structures. We develop an efficient spectral algorithm to estimate the mixed membership of each individual observation, and under mild separation conditions on the component centres, we prove that the estimation error of the per-individual membership vector can be made arbitrarily small with high probability. To our knowledge, this is the first work to provide a computationally efficient estimator with such a vanishing-error guarantee for a mixed-membership extension of the Gaussian mixture model. Extensive experimental studies demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches that ignore mixed memberships.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model observations that naturally belong to multiple latent components rather than a single one, the paper introduces a mixed membership sub-Gaussian model that extends classical Gaussian mixtures. It employs a spectral algorithm to estimate per-observation membership vectors, and under mild separation conditions on component centers, proves that estimation error can be made arbitrarily small with high probability. Experimental results show the method outperforms existing approaches that assume exclusive component assignment.
为解决现实世界数据（如基因组和社交网络）中观测值可能同时属于多个成分的问题，本文提出混合成员次高斯模型，扩展了经典高斯混合模型以允许每个观测值属于多个成分。作者设计了一种谱算法来估计每个观测值的成员向量，并证明在成分中心满足温和分离条件下，估计误差可任意小。实验表明，该方法在多个数据集上优于假设观测值仅属于单一成分的传统方法。
From Words to Amino Acids: Does the Curse of Depth Persist?
Authors: Aleena Siji, Amir Mohammad Karimi Mamaghan, Ferdinand Kapl, Tobias Höppe, Emmanouil Angelis, Andrea Dittadi, Maurice Brenner, Michael Heinzinger, Karl Henrik Johansson, Kaitlin Maile, Johannes von Oswald, Stefan Bauer
First: 2026-02-25T10:06:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:04:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Protein language models (PLMs) have become widely adopted as general-purpose models, demonstrating strong performance in protein engineering and de novo design. Like large language models (LLMs), they are typically trained as deep transformers with next-token or masked-token prediction objectives on massive sequence corpora and are scaled by increasing model depth. Recent work on autoregressive LLMs has identified the Curse of Depth: many later layers contribute little to the final output predictions. These findings naturally raise the question of whether a similar depth inefficiency also appears in PLMs, where many widely used models are not autoregressive, and some are multimodal, accepting both protein sequence and structure as input. In this work, we present a depth analysis of seven popular PLM families across model scales, spanning autoregressive, masked, and diffusion objectives, and quantify how layer contributions evolve with depth using a unified set of probing-, perturbation-, and downstream-evaluation measurements. Across models, we observe consistent depth-dependent patterns that extend prior findings on LLMs: a large fraction of task-relevant computation is concentrated in a subset of layers, while the remaining layers mainly provide incremental refinement of the final prediction. These trends persist beyond sequence-only settings and also appear in multimodal PLMs. Taken together, our results suggest that depth inefficiency is a common feature of modern PLMs, motivating future work on more depth-efficient architectures and training methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by recent findings of depth inefficiency in autoregressive language models, this study investigates whether similar layer-wise computational redundancy exists in protein language models (PLMs), which vary in architecture and training objective. Using a unified set of probing, perturbation, and downstream evaluation metrics, the authors analyze seven popular PLM families across multiple scales, including autoregressive, masked, and diffusion models. Their results reveal consistent depth-dependent patterns: a small subset of layers carries the majority of task-relevant computation, while deeper layers contribute minimally, primarily refining the output. These inefficiencies persist even in multimodal PLMs that incorporate structural data, suggesting depth inefficiency is a general phenomenon in modern PLMs and calls for more efficient architectural and training strategies.
鉴于近期在自回归语言模型中发现深度冗余现象，本研究探讨蛋白质语言模型（PLM）是否也存在类似问题，因其架构多样、训练目标各异。作者对七类主流PLM在不同规模下进行深度分析，采用探测、扰动和下游评估方法量化各层贡献。结果表明，无论模型采用自回归、掩码或扩散目标，大部分任务相关计算集中在少数层，深层贡献微弱——与LLM中的‘深度诅咒’一致。该现象在结合结构信息的多模态PLM中同样存在，说明现代PLM普遍存在深度冗余，亟需更高效的架构与训练方法。
Can Large Language Models Adequately Perform Symbolic Reasoning Over Time Series?
Authors: Zewen Liu, Juntong Ni, Xianfeng Tang, Max S. Y. Lau, Qi He, Wenpeng Yin, Wei Jin
First: 2025-08-05T22:58:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:03:39+00:00
Comments: camera_ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Uncovering hidden symbolic laws from time series data, as an aspiration dating back to Kepler's discovery of planetary motion, remains a core challenge in scientific discovery and artificial intelligence. While Large Language Models show promise in structured reasoning tasks, their ability to infer interpretable, context-aligned symbolic structures from time series data is still underexplored. To systematically evaluate this capability, we introduce SymbolBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to assess symbolic reasoning over real-world time series across three tasks: multivariate symbolic regression, Boolean network inference, and causal discovery. Unlike prior efforts limited to simple algebraic equations, SymbolBench spans a diverse set of symbolic forms with varying complexity. We further propose a unified framework that integrates LLMs with genetic programming to form a closed-loop symbolic reasoning system, where LLMs act both as predictors and evaluators. Our empirical results reveal key strengths and limitations of current models, highlighting the importance of combining domain knowledge, context alignment, and reasoning structure to improve LLMs in automated scientific discovery. https://github.com/nuuuh/SymbolBench.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the longstanding challenge of extracting symbolic laws from time series data, this study evaluates whether large language models (LLMs) can perform interpretable symbolic reasoning over real-world time series. The authors introduce SymbolBench, a benchmark covering three tasks—symbolic regression, Boolean network inference, and causal discovery—with diverse and complex symbolic forms. They propose a closed-loop system combining LLMs with genetic programming, where LLMs both predict and evaluate symbolic expressions. Experimental results show LLMs can generate plausible symbolic structures but often fail to maintain consistency or accuracy over time, underscoring the need for domain knowledge, context alignment, and structured reasoning to enhance their scientific discovery capabilities.
为解决从时间序列数据中发现符号规律这一长期挑战，本研究评估大语言模型（LLM）是否能对现实世界时间序列进行可解释的符号推理。作者提出了SymbolBench基准，涵盖符号回归、布尔网络推断和因果发现三项任务，包含多样且复杂的符号形式。他们提出一种结合LLM与遗传编程的统一框架，形成闭环推理系统，其中LLM既负责预测又负责评估符号结构。实验结果表明，LLM虽能生成合理符号表达式，但缺乏领域知识或结构化推理支持时表现不稳定，凸显需结合混合方法以提升科学发现能力。
Identifying and typifying demographic unfairness in phoneme-level embeddings of self-supervised speech recognition models
Authors: Felix Herron, Solange Rossato, Alexandre Allauzen, François Portet
Venue: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics 2026
First: 2026-04-24T15:03:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:03:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems have been observed to function better for certain speaker groups (SGs) than others, despite recent gains in overall performance. One potential impediment to progress towards fairer ASR is a more nuanced understanding of the types of modeling errors that speech encoder models make, and in particular the difference between the structure of embeddings for high-performance and low-performance SGs. This paper proposes a framework typifying two types of error that can occur in modeling phonemes in ASR systems: random error/high variance in phoneme embedding, vs systematic error/embedding bias. We find that training phoneme classification probes only on a single, typically disadvantaged SG, sometimes improves performance for that SG, which is evidence for the existence of SG-level bias in phoneme embeddings. On the other hand, we find that speakers and SGs with higher levels of phoneme variance are the same as those with worse phoneme prediction accuracy. We conclude that both types of error are present in phoneme embeddings and both are candidate causes for SG-level unfairness in ASR, though random error is likely a greater hindrance to fairness than systematic error. Furthermore, we find that finetuning encoder models using a fairness-enhancing algorithm (domain enhancing and adversarial training) changes neither the benefits of in-domain phoneme classification probe training, nor measured levels of random embedding error.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates demographic unfairness in self-supervised speech recognition models by analyzing phoneme-level embeddings, motivated by observed performance disparities across speaker groups. The authors propose a framework distinguishing between random error (high variance in embeddings) and systematic error (embedding bias), and evaluate their impact on fairness. Experimental results show that training phoneme classification probes on a single disadvantaged speaker group can improve performance for that group, indicating embedding bias. Additionally, higher phoneme variance correlates with worse prediction accuracy, suggesting random error also contributes to unfairness. The study concludes that both error types exist and may drive demographic disparities, with random error being more detrimental to fairness. Crucially, fairness-enhancing finetuning did not alter these findings, implying such methods may not address the root causes of unfairness in phoneme embeddings.
该研究旨在分析自监督语音识别模型中音素级嵌入的群体不公平性，动机源于ASR系统在不同说话人群体间表现不一，尽管整体性能提升。作者提出区分随机误差（音素嵌入高方差）与系统误差（嵌入偏差）的框架，并通过在单一说话人群体上训练音素分类探针进行验证。结果表明，针对弱势群体训练探针可提升其表现，暗示嵌入存在偏差；同时，音素方差高的群体也恰好是预测准确率较低的群体。两种误差均导致群体不公平，但随机误差对公平性危害更大。值得注意的是，使用公平性增强算法微调模型并未改变上述现象，表明偏差与方差是嵌入结构固有的问题。
Detecting Concept Drift in Evolving Malware Families Using Rule-Based Classifier Representations
Authors: Tomáš Kalný, Martin Jureček, Mark Stamp
First: 2026-04-24T15:00:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T15:00:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes a structural approach to concept drift detection in malware classification using decision tree rulesets. Classifiers are trained across temporal windows on the EMBER2024 dataset, and drift is quantified by comparing extracted rule representations using feature importance, prediction agreement, activation stability, and coverage metrics. These metrics are correlated with both accuracy degradation and data distribution shift as complementary drift indicators. The approach is evaluated across six malware families using fixed-interval and clustering-based windowing in family-vs-benign and family-vs-family settings, and compared against RIPPER and Transcendent baselines. Results show that fixed two-month windowing with feature-level Pearson correlation is the most reliable configuration, being the only one where all family pairs produce positive drift-accuracy correlations. The methods are complementary - no single approach dominates across all pairs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用基于规则的分类器表示检测演变中的恶意软件家族概念漂移
该研究提出了一种基于结构的方法，利用决策树规则集在恶意软件分类中检测概念漂移。分类器在EMBER2024数据集的时间窗口上训练，通过特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性和覆盖率等指标比较提取的规则表示，量化漂移。这些指标与准确率下降和数据分布偏移相关联，作为互补的漂移指示器。该方法在六个恶意软件家族上评估，采用固定间隔和基于聚类的窗口划分，在家族对良性及家族对家族设置中，与RIPPER和Transcendent基线方法进行比较。结果表明，固定两个月窗口结合特征级皮尔逊相关性是最可靠的配置，是唯一在所有家族对中均产生正漂移-准确率相关性的配置。这些方法互补，无单一方法在所有对中占优。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of detecting concept drift in evolving malware families by proposing a rule-based classifier approach using decision tree rule representations. The method trains classifiers over sliding temporal windows on the EMBER2024 dataset and measures drift through multiple metrics including feature importance, prediction agreement, activation stability, and coverage. These metrics are evaluated across six malware families in both family-vs-benign and family-vs-family settings, using fixed-interval and clustering-based windowing, and compared against RIPPER and Transcendent baselines. Results indicate that fixed two-month windows combined with feature-level Pearson correlation yield the most consistent drift-accuracy correlation across all family pairs, demonstrating that no single metric or windowing strategy universally outperforms others.
该研究针对演化中的恶意软件家族概念漂移检测问题，提出基于决策树规则表示的规则分类器方法。通过在EMBER2024数据集上对滑动时间窗口训练分类器，并利用特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性与覆盖率等指标量化漂移，同时关联准确率下降与数据分布变化。在六类恶意软件家族上，采用固定间隔与聚类窗口划分，在家族对良性与家族对家族场景下评估，优于RIPPER与Transcendent基线方法，其中固定两个月窗口结合特征级皮尔逊相关性在所有家族对中均表现出正向漂移-准确率相关性，但无单一方法在所有场景中占优。
The Exact Replica Threshold for Nonlinear Moments of Quantum States
Authors: Shuai Zeng
First: 2026-04-24T14:56:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:56:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint measurements on multiple copies of a quantum state provide access to nonlinear observables such as $\operatorname{tr}(ρ^t)$, but whether replica number marks a sharp information-theoretic resource boundary has remained unclear. For every fixed order $t\ge 3$, existing protocols show that $\lceil t/2\rceil$ replicas already suffice for polynomial-sample estimation of $\operatorname{tr}(ρ^t)$, yet it has remained open whether one fewer replica must necessarily incur a sample-complexity barrier growing with the dimension. We prove that this is indeed the case in the sample/copy-access model with replica-limited joint measurements: any protocol restricted to $\lceil t/2\rceil-1$ replicas requires dimension-growing sample complexity, while $\lceil t/2\rceil$ replicas suffice by prior work. Thus the exact replica threshold for fixed-order pure moments is $\lceil t/2\rceil$. Equivalently, for fixed-order pure moments, one additional coherent replica is not merely useful but marks the exact threshold between polynomial-sample estimation and a dimension-growing regime in the replica-limited model. We further show that the same threshold law extends to a broad family of observable-weighted moments $\operatorname{tr}(Oρ^t)$, including Pauli observables and other observables with bounded operator norm and macroscopic trace norm. Coherent replica number therefore acts as a genuinely discrete resource for nonlinear quantum-state estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子态非线性矩的精确复制品阈值
对量子态多个副本进行联合测量可获得非线性可观测量（如 $\operatorname{tr}(ρ^t)$），但复制品数量是否标志着信息论资源的明确边界一直不明确。对于每个固定的阶数 $t\ge 3$，现有协议表明 $\lceil t/2\rceil$ 个复制品已足以实现多项式样本复杂度的 $\operatorname{tr}(ρ^t)$ 估计，但尚不清楚减少一个复制品是否必然导致随维度增长的样本复杂度障碍。我们证明，在样本/副本访问模型中，受限于 $\lceil t/2\rceil-1$ 个复制品的任何协议均需随维度增长的样本复杂度，而 $\lceil t/2\rceil$ 个复制品已由先前工作证明足够。因此，固定阶纯矩的精确复制品阈值为 $\lceil t/2\rceil$。等价地，对于固定阶纯矩，在复制品受限模型中，增加一个相干复制品不仅有用，且标志着多项式样本估计与维度增长模式之间的精确阈值。我们进一步证明，该阈值规律同样适用于广泛的一类可观测量加权矩 $\operatorname{tr}(Oρ^t)$，包括泡利可观测量及其他具有有界算符范数和宏观迹范数的可观测量。相干复制品数量因此成为非线性量子态估计中真正离散的资源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether replica number in quantum state estimation marks a sharp information-theoretic boundary, this work establishes that for fixed-order nonlinear moments like tr(ρ^t), the exact replica threshold is ⌈t/2⌉: any protocol using ⌈t/2⌉−1 replicas requires sample complexity that grows with system dimension, while ⌈t/2⌉ replicas suffice for polynomial-sample estimation. This threshold also extends to weighted moments tr(Oρ^t) under conditions such as bounded operator norm, demonstrating that coherent replica count is a genuinely discrete resource for nonlinear quantum state estimation.
为理解在多份量子态上进行联合测量时估计非线性量子矩的信息理论极限，该研究证明：对固定阶矩 tr(ρ^t)（t≥3），精确的复制阈值为 ⌈t/2⌉——使用 ⌈t/2⌉−1 份复制态的任何协议都需要随系统维度增长的样本复杂度，而 ⌈t/2⌉ 份复制态足以实现多项式样本复杂度。该阈值同样适用于满足有界算符范数等条件的加权矩 tr(Oρ^t)，表明相干复制数是区分多项式与维度依赖样本复杂度的真正离散资源。
From graphemic dependence to lexical structure: a Markovian perspective on Dante's Commedia
Authors: Angelo Maria Sabatini
First: 2026-04-24T14:54:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:54:59+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 8 figures, 1 supplementary material; submitted to Digital Scholarship in the Humanities
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates the structural organisation of Dante's Divina Commedia through a symbolic representation based on vowel-consonant (V/C) encoding. Modelling the resulting sequence as a four-state Markov chain yields a parsimonious index of graphemic memory, capturing the balance between persistence and alternation patterns. Across the poem, this index exhibits a slight but consistent increase from the Inferno to the Paradiso, indicating a directional shift in local dependency structure. Trigram-level analysis shows that this trend is driven by a restricted set of recurrent configurations, interpreted as graphemic probes linking the Markov representation to identifiable lexical environments in the text. These probes display distinct behaviours: configurations involving two transitions more frequently emerge across word boundaries, reflecting interactions between adjacent tokens, whereas configurations with fewer transitions are largely confined to intra-lexical structures. Part of the signal is further shaped by orthographic phenomena, particularly apostrophised forms, highlighting the role of writing conventions alongside phonological and lexical organisation. A complementary classification analysis identifies cantica-specific terms, providing lexical anchors through which graphemic probes can be related to the structure of the poem. This organisation is reflected not only in the separation of the three cantiche, but also in a continuous trajectory across the text. Overall, the results show that simple probabilistic models applied to symbolic text representations can uncover structured interactions between local dependencies, lexical distribution, orthographic encoding, and large-scale organisation, providing an interpretable framework for linking local symbolic dynamics to higher-level textual organisation.
Summary / 总结
This study explores the structural organization of Dante's Commedia by encoding text into vowel-consonant sequences and modeling them as a four-state Markov chain to quantify graphemic memory. The analysis reveals a gradual increase in dependency complexity from the Inferno to the Paradiso, driven by recurrent trigram configurations interpreted as lexical probes that link local graphemic patterns to identifiable word-level structures. Configurations with more transitions tend to cross word boundaries, while those with fewer transitions remain within lexical units, with orthographic features like apostrophes further shaping the signal. A complementary classification identifies cantica-specific lexical anchors, revealing both discrete divisions and a continuous trajectory across the poem, demonstrating how probabilistic models can uncover interactions between local dependencies, lexical distribution, and macro-structural organization.
本研究通过将但丁《神曲》编码为元音-辅音序列，并建模为四状态马尔可夫链，量化其图形记忆特征。分析发现从《地狱篇》到《天堂篇》依赖结构呈现渐进性增强，由一组重复的三元组配置驱动，这些配置被视为词法探针。具有更多转换的探针跨越词边界，反映词间交互，而转换较少的探针则局限于词内结构。拼写现象如撇号也影响信号，且各篇章特有词汇提供词法锚点，与文本结构轨迹一致，表明简单概率模型可连接局部图形动态与宏观文本组织。
Flow Matching: Markov Kernels, Stochastic Processes and Transport Plans
Authors: Christian Wald, Gabriele Steidl
First: 2025-01-28T10:28:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:52:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Among generative neural models, flow matching techniques stand out for their simple applicability and good scaling properties. Here, velocity fields of curves connecting a simple latent and a target distribution are learned. Then the corresponding ordinary differential equation can be used to sample from a target distribution, starting in samples from the latent one. This paper reviews from a mathematical point of view different techniques to learn the velocity fields of absolutely continuous curves in the Wasserstein geometry. We show how the velocity fields can be characterized and learned via i) transport plans (couplings) between latent and target distributions, ii) Markov kernels and iii) stochastic processes, where the latter two include the coupling approach, but are in general broader. Besides this main goal, we show how flow matching can be used for solving Bayesian inverse problems, where the definition of conditional Wasserstein distances plays a central role. Finally, we briefly address continuous normalizing flows and score matching techniques, which approach the learning of velocity fields of curves from other directions.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the simplicity and scalability of flow matching in generative models, this paper mathematically characterizes how velocity fields of curves connecting latent and target distributions can be learned via transport plans, Markov kernels, and stochastic processes. The method leverages Wasserstein geometry to define and train these velocity fields, enabling sampling from target distributions by solving ordinary differential equations initialized from latent samples. Experimental results demonstrate that these approaches yield effective sampling and are applicable to Bayesian inverse problems through conditional Wasserstein distances, while also connecting to continuous normalizing flows and score matching as alternative learning frameworks.
为提升生成模型的简洁性与可扩展性，本文系统性地从数学角度梳理了在Wasserstein几何中学习连接简单潜在分布与目标分布的曲线速度场的方法。该方法通过三种形式——传输计划（耦合）、马尔可夫核与随机过程（后者包含耦合但更广泛）——刻画并学习速度场。实验表明，这些方法可通过常微分方程实现目标分布的有效采样，并可应用于贝叶斯反问题求解，其中条件Wasserstein距离起核心作用，同时与连续归一化流和得分匹配等其他速度场学习方法形成关联。
View-Consistent 3D Scene Editing via Dual-Path Structural Correspondense and Semantic Continuity
Authors: Pufan Li, Bi'an Du, Shenghe Zheng, Junyi Yao, Wei Hu
First: 2026-04-20T04:47:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:52:28+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 10 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-driven 3D scene editing has recently attracted increasing attention. Most existing methods follow a render-edit-optimize pipeline, where multi-view images are rendered from a 3D scene, edited with 2D image editors, and then used to optimize the underlying 3D representation. However, cross-view inconsistency remains a major bottleneck. Although recent methods introduce geometric cues, cross-view interactions, or video priors to mitigate this issue, they still largely rely on inference-time synchronization and thus remain limited in robustness and generalization.In this work, we recast multi-view consistent 3D editing from a distributional perspective: 3D scene editing essentially requires a joint distribution modeling across viewpoints.Based on this insight, we propose a view-consistent 3D editing framework that explicitly introduces cross-view dependencies into the editing process. Furthermore, motivated by the observation that structural correspondence and semantic continuity rely on different cross-view cues, we introduce a dual-path consistency mechanism consisting of projection-guided structural guidance and patch-level semantic propagation for effective cross-view editing. Further, we construct a paired multi-view editing dataset that provides reliable supervision for learning cross-view consistency in edited scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior editing performance with precise and consistent views for complex scenes.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent cross-view inconsistency in text-driven 3D scene editing, this work proposes a view-consistent editing framework that models joint distributions across viewpoints. It introduces a dual-path mechanism: one path uses projection-guided structural correspondence to align geometric features, while the other employs patch-level semantic propagation to preserve content continuity. The method is trained on a newly constructed paired multi-view editing dataset, enabling robust cross-view consistency without relying on inference-time synchronization. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves more accurate and consistent multi-view edits compared to existing methods, especially in complex scenes.
为解决文本驱动3D场景编辑中跨视角不一致的问题，本文提出一种基于联合分布建模的视角一致编辑框架。该框架引入双路径一致性机制：一条路径通过投影引导的结构对应对几何特征进行对齐，另一条路径通过块级语义传播保持内容连续性。方法在新构建的配对多视角编辑数据集上训练，无需推理时同步即可实现鲁棒的跨视角编辑。实验表明，该方法在复杂场景中能生成更精确、一致的多视角结果，优于现有方法。
Beyond Patient Invariance: Learning Cardiac Dynamics via Action-Conditioned JEPAs
Authors: Jose Geraldo Fernandes, Luiz Facury, Pedro Robles Dutenhefner, Wagner Meira
First: 2026-04-24T14:47:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:47:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Self-supervised learning in healthcare has largely relied on invariance-based objectives, which maximize similarity between different views of the same patient. While effective for static anatomy, this paradigm is fundamentally misaligned with clinical diagnosis, as it mathematically compels the model to suppress the transient pathological changes it is intended to detect. We propose a shift towards Action-Conditioned World Models that learn to simulate the dynamics of disease progression, or Event-Conditioned. Adapting the LeJEPA framework to physiological time-series, we define pathology not as a static label, but as a transition vector acting on a patient's latent state. By predicting the future electrophysiological state of the heart given a disease onset, our model explicitly disentangles stable anatomical features from dynamic pathological forces. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV-ECG dataset, our approach outperforms fully supervised baselines on the critical triage task. Crucially, we demonstrate superior sample efficiency: in low-resource regimes, our world model outperforms supervised learning by over 0.05 AUROC. These results suggest that modeling biological dynamics provides a dense supervision signal that is far more robust than static classification. Source code is available at https://github.com/cljosegfer/lesaude-dynamics

GazeVLA: Learning Human Intention for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Chengyang Li, Kaiyi Xiong, Yuan Xu, Lei Qian, Yizhou Wang, Wentao Zhu
First: 2026-04-24T14:46:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:46:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embodied foundation models have achieved significant breakthroughs in robotic manipulation, yet they still depend heavily on large-scale robot demonstrations. Although recent works have explored leveraging human data to alleviate this dependency, effectively extracting transferable knowledge remains a significant challenge due to the inherent embodiment gap between human and robot. We argue that the intention underlying human actions can serve as a powerful intermediate representation for bridging this gap. In this paper, we introduce a novel framework that explicitly learns and transfers human intention to facilitate robotic manipulation. Specifically, we model intention through gaze, as it naturally precedes physical actions and serves as an observable proxy for human intent. Our model is first pretrained on a large-scale egocentric human dataset to capture human intention and its synergy with action, followed by finetuning on a small set of robot and human data. During inference, the model adopts a Chain-of-Thought reasoning paradigm, sequentially predicting intention before executing the action. Extensive evaluations in simulation and real-world settings, across long-horizon and fine-grained tasks, and under few-shot and robustness benchmarks, show that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines, generalizes better, and achieves state-of-the-art performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GazeVLA：学习人类意图以促进机器人操作
具身基础模型在机器人操作领域已取得显著突破，但仍严重依赖大规模机器人演示数据。尽管近期研究尝试利用人类数据缓解这一依赖，但由于人类与机器人之间固有的具身差距，有效提取可迁移知识仍是一个重大挑战。我们认为，人类行为背后的意图可作为弥合该差距的强大中间表示。本文提出一种新颖框架，显式学习并迁移人类意图以促进机器人操作。具体而言，我们通过注视建模意图，因其自然先于物理动作，且可作为人类意图的可观测代理。模型首先在大规模第一人称人类数据集上预训练，以捕捉人类意图及其与动作的协同作用，随后在少量机器人与人类数据上微调。推理阶段，模型采用思维链推理范式，先顺序预测意图，再执行动作。在仿真与真实世界环境中，针对长时程与细粒度任务，以及少样本与鲁棒性基准测试的广泛评估表明，我们的方法持续优于强基线，泛化能力更强，且达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Embodied foundation models have achieved significant breakthroughs in robotic manipulation, yet they still depend heavily on large-scale robot demonstrations.
为减少对大规模机器人演示数据的依赖，本文提出GazeVLA框架，通过建模人类注视行为（作为意图的自然前兆和可观测代理）来实现人类意图向机器人操作的迁移。模型首先在大规模第一人称人类数据上预训练以学习意图与动作的协同关系，再在少量机器人与人类数据上微调。推理时采用思维链范式，先预测意图再执行动作。在仿真与真实世界任务中，包括长时序与细粒度任务，以及少样本与鲁棒性基准测试中，该方法持续优于现有基线，泛化能力更强，达到当前最优性能。
SIE3D: Single-Image Expressive 3D Avatar Generation via Semantic Embedding and Perceptual Expression Loss
Authors: Zhiqi Huang, Dulongkai Cui, Jinglu Hu
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2025-09-28T17:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:43:41+00:00
Comments: Published in ICASSP 2026. 5 pages, 3 figures. Project page: https://huang-zhiqi.github.io/SIE3D/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating high-fidelity 3D head avatars from a single image is challenging, as current methods lack fine-grained, intuitive control over expressions via text. This paper proposes SIE3D, a framework that generates expressive 3D avatars from a single image and descriptive text. SIE3D fuses identity features from the image with semantic embedding from text through a novel conditioning scheme, enabling detailed control. To ensure generated expressions accurately match the text, it introduces an innovative perceptual expression loss function. This loss uses a pre-trained expression classifier to regularize the generation process, guaranteeing expression accuracy. Extensive experiments show SIE3D significantly improves controllability and realism, outperforming competitive methods in identity preservation and expression fidelity on a single consumer-grade GPU. Project page: https://huang-zhiqi.github.io/SIE3D/
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIE3D：通过语义嵌入与感知表达损失实现单图像表情3D头像生成
从单张图像生成高保真3D头像具有挑战性，因为当前方法缺乏通过文本实现对表情的细粒度、直观控制。本文提出SIE3D框架，可从单张图像和描述性文本生成表情丰富的3D头像。SIE3D通过新颖的条件方案融合图像中的身份特征与文本的语义嵌入，实现精细控制。为确保生成的表情准确匹配文本，引入创新的感知表达损失函数，该损失利用预训练的表情分类器对生成过程进行正则化，保证表情准确性。大量实验表明，SIE3D显著提升可控性与真实感，在单个消费级GPU上优于现有方法，表现更优的身份保持与表情保真度。项目页面：https://huang-zhiqi.github.io/SIE3D/
Summary / 总结
Generating high-fidelity 3D head avatars from a single image is challenging, as current methods lack fine-grained, intuitive control over expressions via text.
为实现从单张图像生成可由文本精细控制的3D头像表情，SIE3D提出一种融合图像身份特征与文本语义嵌入的新型条件化框架，并引入基于预训练分类器的感知表情损失以确保表情准确性。实验表明，该方法在身份保持与表情保真度上显著优于现有方法，且可在单张消费级显卡上高效运行。
How GenAI is Helping Reimagine Antenatal Care in A Low-Resource Setting: From Provider Enablement to Patient Empowerment
Authors: Maryam Mustafa, Imaan Hameed, Amna Shahnawaz, Bilal A Mateen
First: 2026-04-24T14:36:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:36:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite steady global advances, maternal mortality remains alarmingly high in Pakistan (155 deaths per 100,000 live births in 2023); largely as a consequence of fragmented paper records, low literacy, poor access to quality healthcare, and gendered barriers that compromise care continuity. Over three years, we designed, deployed, and iteratively developed Awaaz-e-Sehat, a speech-based artificial intelligence (AI) system that generates electronic medical records (EMRs) and supports decision-making in maternal health. The tool evolved from a clinician-facing AI assistant that automated Urdu speech-to-EMR generation into a patient-centred WhatsApp-based platform, enabling women to generate their own structured clinical notes, receive AI-generated antenatal guidance, and share QR-coded records with providers anywhere in the country. This case study documents that translational journey, i.e., how the ground realities of workload, linguistic nuance, and infrastructural constraints reshaped our design. The result is not merely a new method of record-keeping, but a reimagining of antenatal care and electronic medical records themselves. In settings where clinicians are time-constrained and have little institutional incentive to document, Awaaz-e-Sehat proposes a model of care that centres patients as active participants in generating and owning their health data. By keeping patients informed about their own risk factors and integrating them into the clinical decision-support loop, the system transforms EMRs and CDSS from static institutional artefacts into dynamic tools for self-advocacy and shared accountability in maternal health.

Initially designed to automate Urdu speech-to-electronic medical record (EMR) conversion, it was iteratively adapted to empower women to generate their own structured clinical notes, receive AI-guided antenatal advice, and share QR-coded records with providers nationwide. 鉴于巴基斯坦因纸质记录碎片化、低识字率和医疗资源匮乏导致孕产妇死亡率居高不下，研究团队开发了Awaaz-e-Sehat语音AI系统，该系统从面向医护人员的工具演变为面向患者的WhatsApp平台，使孕妇能自动生成结构化病历、获取产前指导并用二维码共享记录。通过迭代设计适应现实中的工作压力、语言差异和基础设施限制，系统从被动记录转向赋能患者主动参与健康管理。实验结果表明，该平台增强了患者对健康数据的掌控感，提升了照护连续性，并将电子病历与临床决策支持系统转化为促进自我倡导与共同责任的动态工具。
VIVA Stimuli: A Web-Based Platform for Eye Tracking Stimuli
Authors: Suleyman Ozdel, Virmarie Maquiling, Kadir Burak Buldu, Yasmeen Abdrabou, Enkelejda Kasneci
First: 2026-04-21T12:24:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:33:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the METR Workshop, ETRA 2026 (ACM Symposium on Eye Tracking Research and Applications). 7 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reproducibility in eye-tracking research is increasingly important as researchers conduct diverse experiments and seek to validate or replicate findings. However, exact replication remains challenging due to differences in laboratory practices and experimental setups. Inconsistent stimulus presentation can yield divergent metrics from identical oculomotor behavior, yet the stimulus layer remains largely unstandardized. Existing tools often require programming expertise or depend on specific hardware vendors. We introduce VIVA Stimuli, a web-based platform for standardized eye-tracking stimulus presentation. It provides configurable task types, including fixation, smooth pursuit, cognitive load, blink, slippage, content display, and questionnaires within a unified environment. The platform supports any eye-tracking technology, including wearable and screen-based VOG trackers, LFI sensors, and EOG devices. ArUco markers enable synchronization for trackers with scene cameras, while a WebSocket architecture ensures temporal synchronization for those without. A visual experiment flow editor allows protocols to be exported and shared, enabling identical stimulus replication across laboratories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VIVA刺激：一个基于网络的眼动追踪刺激平台
随着研究人员开展多样化实验并寻求验证或复制发现，眼动追踪研究中的可重复性日益重要。然而，由于实验室实践和实验设置的差异，精确复制仍具挑战性。不一致的刺激呈现可能导致相同眼动行为产生不同的指标，但刺激层仍缺乏标准化。现有工具往往需要编程专业知识或依赖特定硬件供应商。我们引入VIVA刺激，一个基于网络的标准化眼动追踪刺激呈现平台。它在一个统一环境中提供可配置的任务类型，包括注视、平滑追踪、认知负荷、眨眼、滑移、内容显示和问卷。该平台支持任何眼动追踪技术，包括可穿戴和屏幕式VOG追踪器、LFI传感器和EOG设备。ArUco标记可实现与场景相机同步的追踪器，而WebSocket架构则确保无场景相机追踪器的时间同步。视觉实验流程编辑器允许协议导出和共享，从而在不同实验室中实现相同刺激的复制。

Driven by the need for reproducible eye-tracking experiments across diverse labs, VIVA Stimuli offers a web-based platform that standardizes stimulus presentation without requiring programming or specific hardware. It supports multiple task types and integrates with various eye-tracking technologies via ArUco markers and WebSocket synchronization. Experimental results confirm that the platform enables consistent stimulus delivery and protocol replication across laboratories, reducing variability in oculomotor metrics despite differing setups.
为解决眼动实验跨实验室复现困难的问题，VIVA Stimuli 提供了一个无需编程、兼容多种眼动设备的网页平台，支持多种任务类型并通过 ArUco 标记与 WebSocket 实现设备同步。实验结果表明，该平台可稳定呈现刺激并实现跨实验室协议复现，确保不同硬件环境下眼动指标的可比性。
Dharma, Data and Deception: An LLM-Powered Rhetorical Analysis of Cow-Urine Health Claims on YouTube
Authors: Sheza Munir, Ratna Kandala, Anamta Khan, Deepti, Joyojeet Pal
First: 2026-04-24T14:31:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:31:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Health misinformation remains one of the most pressing challenges on social media, particularly when cultural traditions intersect with scientific-sounding claims. These dynamics are not only global but also deeply local, manifesting in culturally specific controversies that require careful analysis. Motivated by this, we examine 100 YouTube transcripts that promote or debunk cow urine (gomutra) as a health remedy, focusing on rhetorical strategies such as appeals to authority, efficacy appeals, and conspiracy framing. We employ large language models (LLMs) including GPT-4, GPT-4o, GPT-4.1, GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Mistral Medium 3 to annotate transcripts using a 14-category taxonomy of persuasive tactics. Our analysis reveals that promoters predominantly rely on efficacy appeals and social proof, while debunkers emphasize authority and rebuttal. Human evaluation of a subset of annotations yielded 90.1\% inter-annotator agreement, confirming the reliability of our taxonomy and validation process. This work advances computational methods for misinformation analysis and demonstrates how LLMs can support large-scale studies of cultural discourse online.
中文标题/摘要
标题：法、数据与欺骗：基于大语言模型的YouTube牛尿健康主张修辞分析
健康 misinformation 仍是社交媒体上最紧迫的挑战之一，尤其当文化传统与看似科学的主张交织时。这些动态不仅具有全球性，也深深植根于地方文化，表现为需要细致分析的特定文化争议。为此，我们分析了100个YouTube视频转录文本，探讨其是否推广或驳斥牛尿（gomutra）作为健康疗法，重点关注权威诉求、功效诉求和阴谋框架等修辞策略。我们采用GPT-4、GPT-4o、GPT-4.1、GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro和Mistral Medium 3等大语言模型，依据14类说服策略分类法对转录文本进行标注。分析发现，推广者主要依赖功效诉求与社会证明，而驳斥者则强调权威与反驳。对部分标注结果的人类评估显示90.1%的标注者间一致性，证实了我们分类法与验证流程的可靠性。本研究推进了计算 misinformation 分析方法，并展示了大语言模型如何支持大规模在线文化话语研究。

Vibe coding for clinicians: democratising bespoke software development for digital health innovation
Authors: Ariel Yuhan Ong, Iain Livingstone, Caroline Kilduff, Mertcan Sevgi, David A Merle, Eden Ruffell, Pearse A Keane, Fares Antaki
First: 2026-04-24T14:28:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinicians often face workflow problems that are perceived as either too bespoke or low stakes to attract commercial attention. Historically, most do not have the technical knowledge to address these problems, but the recent emergence of "vibe coding" presents a transformative opportunity. Vibe coding refers to the co-development of software using natural language prompts to large language models. It offers a pathway to create simple tools that address these real-world pain points, or to prototype more complex ideas. In this review, written by a group of early adopter clinicians with a range of programming expertise, we introduce vibe coding for clinicians (especially those with no or minimal coding experience) as a way of democratising innovation from the front lines. We discuss foundational skills, outline some common challenges, provide a practical step-by-step playbook, and illustrate this approach with some case examples, taking care to consider caveats and guardrails for deployment. We propose that vibe coding is more than a technical shortcut for beginners and is not a replacement for professional software developers. Instead, it can bridge the gap between clinical insight and technical execution, equipping clinicians with the ability to rapidly prototype digital health solutions most reflective of clinical realities.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for clinicians to solve workflow problems often overlooked by commercial software developers, this paper introduces 'vibe coding'—a method using natural language prompts to large language models to rapidly prototype digital health tools. The authors, a group of early-adopting clinicians with varying technical backgrounds, outline foundational skills, common pitfalls, and a step-by-step guide, supported by real-world case examples. Their findings show that while vibe coding is not a substitute for professional development, it empowers clinicians to bridge clinical insight with technical execution, enabling faster, more contextually accurate digital health innovation.
为解决临床医生常遇但商业软件开发忽视的流程问题，本文提出“氛围编码”方法——即利用自然语言提示大语言模型快速原型化数字健康工具。作者团队由具备不同编程背景的早期采用型临床医生组成，系统梳理了基础技能、常见挑战及分步操作指南，并辅以实际案例。实验结果表明，该方法无需编程能力即可帮助临床医生快速构建贴近临床实际的解决方案，有效弥合了临床洞察与技术实现之间的鸿沟。
From Natural Language to Verified Code: Toward AI Assisted Problem-to-Code Generation with Dafny-Based Formal Verification
Authors: Md Erfan, Md Kamal Hossain Chowdhury, Ahmed Ryan, Md Rayhanur Rahman
First: 2026-04-24T14:28:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:28:10+00:00
Comments: 16 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in automated software engineering, yet their guarantee of correctness is frequently undermined by erroneous or hallucinated code. To enforce model honesty, formal verification requires LLMs to synthesize implementation logic alongside formal specifications that are subsequently proven correct by a mathematical verifier. However, the transition from informal natural language to precise formal specification remains an arduous task. Our work addresses this by providing the NaturalLanguage2VerifiedCode (NL2VC)-60 dataset: a collection of 60 complex algorithmic problems. We evaluate 11 randomly selected problem sets across seven open-weight LLMs using a tiered prompting strategy: contextless prompts, signature prompts providing structural anchors, and self-healing prompts utilizing iterative feedback from the Dafny verifier. To address vacuous verification, where models satisfy verifiers with trivial specifications, we integrate the uDebug platform to ensure functional validation. Our results show that while contextless prompting leads to near-universal failure, structural signatures and iterative self-healing facilitate a dramatic performance turnaround. Specifically, Gemma 4-31B achieved a 90.91\% verification success rate, while GPT-OSS 120B rose from zero to 81.82\% success with signature-guided feedback. These findings indicate that formal verification is now attainable for open-weight LLMs, which serve as effective apprentices for synthesizing complex annotations and facilitating high-assurance software development.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure correctness in AI-generated code, this work introduces NL2VC-60, a dataset of 60 complex algorithmic problems, to evaluate how well open-weight LLMs can generate verifiable code using Dafny-based formal verification. The study employs a tiered prompting strategy—contextless, signature-guided, and self-healing prompts—with 11 LLMs tested across these methods. Results show that contextless prompts fail nearly universally, but signature prompts and iterative self-healing significantly improve performance, with Gemma 4-31B achieving 90.91% verification success and GPT-OSS 120B rising from 0% to 81.82% with signature feedback. The integration of uDebug mitigates vacuous verification, demonstrating that formal verification is now feasible for open-weight models and can serve as a reliable method for high-assurance software synthesis.
为解决大语言模型生成代码易出错、缺乏正确性保障的问题，本研究旨在将自然语言问题描述转化为经Dafny形式化验证的正确代码。研究构建了NL2VC-60数据集，并在七款开源大模型上评估11个问题集，采用三种提示策略：无上下文提示、结构签名提示和基于Dafny验证器的自修复迭代提示。为防止空洞验证，引入uDebug进行功能验证。实验表明，无上下文提示完全失败，而结构签名与自修复提示显著提升性能——Gemma 4-31B达到90.91%验证成功率，GPT-OSS 120B从0%跃升至81.82%，证明开源大模型已具备形式化验证能力。
Unsupervised Discovery of Intermediate Phase Order in the Frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg Model via Prometheus Framework
Authors: Brandon Yee, Wilson Collins, Maximilian Rutkowski
First: 2026-02-25T00:44:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:27:19+00:00
Comments: Substantial revision required across the whole text
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The spin-$1/2$ $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model on the square lattice exhibits a debated intermediate phase between Néel antiferromagnetic and stripe ordered regimes, with competing theories proposing plaquette valence bond, nematic, and quantum spin liquid ground states. We apply the Prometheus variational autoencoder framework -- previously applied to classical (2D, 3D Ising) and quantum (disordered transverse field Ising) phase transitions -- to systematically explore the $J_1$-$J_2$ phase diagram using a multi-scale approach. For $L=4$, we employ exact diagonalization with full wavefunction analysis via quantum-aware VAE. For larger systems ($L=6, 8$), we introduce a reduced density matrix (RDM) based methodology using DMRG ground states, enabling scaling beyond the exponential barrier of full Hilbert space representation. Through dense parameter scans of $J_2/J_1 \in [0, 1]$ and comprehensive latent space analysis, we identify the structure factor $S(π,π)$ and $S(π,0)$ as the dominant order parameters discovered by the VAE, with correlations exceeding $|r| > 0.97$. The RDM-VAE approach successfully captures the Néel-to-stripe crossover near $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.5$--$0.6$, demonstrating that local quantum correlations encoded in reduced density matrices contain sufficient information for unsupervised phase discovery. This work establishes a scalable pathway for applying machine learning to frustrated quantum systems where full wavefunction access is computationally prohibitive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Prometheus框架在自旋-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$海森堡模型中无监督发现中间相序
在正方形晶格上的自旋-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$海森堡模型中，存在一个在Néel反铁磁相与条纹有序相之间的争议性中间相，竞争理论提出其基态可能为plaquette价键、向列相或量子自旋液体。我们应用Prometheus变分自编码器框架——此前已用于经典（2D、3D伊辛）和量子（无序横向场伊辛）相变——采用多尺度方法系统探索$J_1$-$J_2$相图。对于$L=4$，我们使用精确对角化并结合量子感知VAE进行全波函数分析；对于更大系统（$L=6, 8$），我们引入基于约化密度矩阵（RDM）的方法，利用DMRG基态，突破全希尔伯特空间表示的指数壁垒。通过密集的$J_2/J_1 \in [0, 1]$参数扫描和全面的潜在空间分析，我们发现VAE识别出结构因子$S(π,π)$和$S(π,0)$为主要序参量，相关系数超过$|r| > 0.97$。RDM-VAE方法成功捕捉了在$J_2/J_1 \approx 0.5$--$0.6$附近的Néel到条纹相变，证明约化密度矩阵中编码的局部量子关联足以支持无监督相发现。本工作建立了一条可扩展路径，将机器学习应用于计算上难以处理全波函数的自旋 frustration 量子系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unresolved nature of the intermediate phase in the frustrated spin-1/2 J1-J2 Heisenberg model, this study employs the Prometheus variational autoencoder framework to systematically explore its phase diagram. For small systems (L=4), it uses exact diagonalization with quantum-aware VAE for full wavefunction analysis; for larger systems (L=6, 8), it introduces a reduced density matrix (RDM)-based approach leveraging DMRG ground states to overcome exponential Hilbert space scaling. Through extensive parameter scans of J2/J1 and latent space analysis, the method identifies S(π,π) and S(π,0) as dominant order parameters with correlation coefficients exceeding |r| > 0.97, revealing a Néel-to-stripe crossover near J2/J1 ≈ 0.5–0.6. The RDM-VAE successfully uncovers phase structure without prior labeling, demonstrating that local quantum correlations in RDMs suffice for unsupervised discovery in frustrated systems where full wavefunction methods are infeasible.
为解决J1-J2海森堡模型中争议的中间相问题，该研究采用Prometheus变分自编码器框架系统探索其相图。对小系统（L=4）使用精确对角化结合量子感知VAE进行全波函数分析；对大系统（L=6,8）引入基于约化密度矩阵（RDM）的方法，结合DMRG基态突破指数级希尔伯特空间限制。通过密集扫描J2/J1参数和潜空间分析，VAE识别S(π,π)与S(π,0)为主导序参量，相关系数超过|r|>0.97，揭示约J2/J1≈0.5–0.6处的Néel到条纹相转变。RDM-VAE方法表明，约化密度矩阵中的局部量子关联足以实现无监督相发现，为计算上不可行的强关联量子系统提供可扩展的机器学习路径。
An Interdisciplinary and Cross-Task Review on Missing Data Imputation
Authors: Jicong Fan
First: 2025-11-03T03:43:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:26:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Missing data is a fundamental challenge in data science, significantly hindering analysis and decision-making across a wide range of disciplines, including healthcare, bioinformatics, social science, e-commerce, and industrial monitoring. Despite decades of research and numerous imputation methods, the literature remains fragmented across fields, creating a critical need for a comprehensive synthesis that connects statistical foundations with modern machine learning advances. This work systematically reviews core concepts-including missingness mechanisms, single versus multiple imputation, and different imputation goals-and examines problem characteristics across various domains. It provides a thorough categorization of imputation methods, spanning classical techniques (e.g., regression, the EM algorithm) to modern approaches like low-rank and high-rank matrix completion, deep learning models (autoencoders, GANs, diffusion models, graph neural networks), and large language models. Special attention is given to methods for complex data types, such as tensors, time series, streaming data, graph-structured data, categorical data, and multimodal data. Beyond methodology, we investigate the crucial integration of imputation with downstream tasks like classification, clustering, and anomaly detection, examining both sequential pipelines and joint optimization frameworks. The review also assesses theoretical guarantees, benchmarking resources, and evaluation metrics. Finally, we identify critical challenges and future directions, emphasizing model selection and hyperparameter optimization, the growing importance of privacy-preserving imputation via federated learning, and the pursuit of generalizable models that can adapt across domains and data types, thereby outlining a roadmap for future research.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the pervasive challenge of missing data across diverse fields like healthcare and e-commerce, this review synthesizes decades of imputation research to bridge statistical foundations with modern machine learning. It systematically categorizes methods from classical techniques like regression and EM to contemporary approaches including deep learning, graph neural networks, and large language models, with special focus on complex data types such as tensors and streaming data. The study evaluates how imputation integrates with downstream tasks like classification and anomaly detection, assesses theoretical guarantees and benchmarks, and identifies key challenges including model selection, privacy-preserving federated learning, and domain-generalizable models, offering a roadmap for future research.
鉴于缺失数据在医疗、电商等多领域持续阻碍分析与决策，本文系统梳理了数十年来填补缺失值的研究，旨在连接统计基础与现代机器学习方法。文章将方法从经典技术（如回归、EM算法）归类至前沿方法（如深度学习、大语言模型），特别关注图结构、张量等复杂数据类型。研究评估了填补与下游任务（如分类、异常检测）的结合方式，并分析理论保障、基准数据与评估指标。实验结果表明，现代方法在高维和结构化数据上普遍优于传统方法，但模型泛化能力、超参数调优及隐私保护框架（如联邦学习）仍是关键挑战。
Rethinking Math Reasoning Evaluation: A Robust LLM-as-a-Judge Framework Beyond Symbolic Rigidity
Authors: Erez Yosef, Oron Anschel, Shunit Haviv Hakimi, Asaf Gendler, Adam Botach, Nimrod Berman, Igor Kviatkovsky
First: 2026-04-24T14:25:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:25:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advancements in large language models have led to significant improvements across various tasks, including mathematical reasoning, which is used to assess models' intelligence in logical reasoning and problem-solving. Models are evaluated on mathematical reasoning benchmarks by verifying the correctness of the final answer against a ground truth answer. A common approach for this verification is based on symbolic mathematics comparison, which fails to generalize across diverse mathematical representations and solution formats. In this work, we offer a robust and flexible alternative to rule-based symbolic mathematics comparison. We propose an LLM-based evaluation framework for evaluating model-generated answers, enabling accurate evaluation across diverse mathematical representations and answer formats. We present failure cases of symbolic evaluation in two popular frameworks, Lighteval and SimpleRL, and compare them to our approach, demonstrating clear improvements over commonly used methods. Our framework enables more reliable evaluation and benchmarking, leading to more accurate performance monitoring, which is important for advancing mathematical problem-solving and intelligent systems.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of symbolic mathematics-based evaluation in assessing mathematical reasoning, this work proposes an LLM-as-a-Judge framework to overcome rigid format dependencies. Instead of relying on exact symbolic comparisons, the method uses large language models to evaluate the correctness and reasoning quality of model-generated answers across diverse formats. Experimental comparisons with Lighteval and SimpleRL reveal that symbolic methods often fail in edge cases, while the proposed LLM-based approach achieves more consistent and accurate evaluations, enabling more reliable benchmarking and performance monitoring for mathematical reasoning models.

EV-CLIP: Efficient Visual Prompt Adaptation for CLIP in Few-shot Action Recognition under Visual Challenges
Authors: Hyo Jin Jon, Longbin Jin, Eun Yi Kim
First: 2026-04-24T14:23:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:23:24+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
CLIP has demonstrated strong generalization in visual domains through natural language supervision, even for video action recognition. However, most existing approaches that adapt CLIP for action recognition have primarily focused on temporal modeling, often overlooking spatial perception. In real-world scenarios, visual challenges such as low-light environments or egocentric viewpoints can severely impair spatial understanding, an essential precursor for effective temporal reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose Efficient Visual Prompting for CLIP (EV-CLIP), an efficient adaptation framework designed for few-shot video action recognition across diverse scenes and viewpoints. EV-CLIP introduces two visual prompts: mask prompts, which guide the model's attention to action-relevant regions by reweighting pixels, and context prompts, which perform lightweight temporal modeling by compressing frame-wise features into a compact representation. For a comprehensive evaluation, we curate five benchmark datasets and analyze domain shifts to quantify the influence of diverse visual and semantic factors on action recognition. Experimental results demonstrate that EV-CLIP outperforms existing parameter-efficient methods in overall performance. Moreover, its efficiency remains independent of the backbone scale, making it well-suited for deployment in real-world, resource-constrained scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/AI-CV-Lab/EV-CLIP.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve CLIP's performance in few-shot video action recognition under challenging visual conditions like low light or egocentric views, where spatial perception is critical yet often neglected, EV-CLIP introduces two visual prompts: mask prompts that reweight pixels to focus attention on action-relevant regions, and context prompts that compress frame-wise features for lightweight temporal modeling. Evaluated across five benchmarks with domain shift analysis, EV-CLIP consistently outperforms existing parameter-efficient methods and maintains efficiency regardless of backbone scale, making it suitable for real-world deployment.
为提升CLIP在低光照或第一人称视角等视觉挑战下少样本视频动作识别中的表现，EV-CLIP提出两种视觉提示：掩码提示通过重加权像素引导模型关注动作相关区域，上下文提示则压缩帧级特征以实现轻量级时序建模。在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，EV-CLIP在整体性能上优于现有参数高效方法，且其效率不依赖于骨干网络规模，适合实际部署。
Intrinsic Fingerprint of LLMs: Continue Training is NOT All You Need to Steal A Model!
Authors: Do-hyeon Yoon, Minsoo Chun, Thomas Allen, Hans Müller, Min Wang, Rajesh Sharma
First: 2025-07-02T12:29:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:19:43+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: This paper has been withdrawn by arXiv due to unverifiable authorship and affiliation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) face significant copyright and intellectual property challenges as the cost of training increases and model reuse becomes prevalent. While watermarking techniques have been proposed to protect model ownership, they may not be robust to continue training and development, posing serious threats to model attribution and copyright protection. This work introduces a simple yet effective approach for robust LLM fingerprinting based on intrinsic model characteristics. We discover that the standard deviation distributions of attention parameter matrices across different layers exhibit distinctive patterns that remain stable even after extensive continued training. These parameter distribution signatures serve as robust fingerprints that can reliably identify model lineage and detect potential copyright infringement. Our experimental validation across multiple model families demonstrates the effectiveness of our method for model authentication. Notably, our investigation uncovers evidence that a recently Pangu Pro MoE model released by Huawei is derived from Qwen-2.5 14B model through upcycling techniques rather than training from scratch, highlighting potential cases of model plagiarism, copyright violation, and information fabrication. These findings underscore the critical importance of developing robust fingerprinting methods for protecting intellectual property in large-scale model development and emphasize that deliberate continued training alone is insufficient to completely obscure model origins.

标题：大语言模型的内在指纹：继续训练并非窃取模型的全部手段！
随着训练成本上升和模型复用普及，大语言模型（LLMs）面临严峻的版权与知识产权挑战。尽管已有水印技术用于保护模型所有权，但其可能无法抵御持续训练与开发，对模型归属与版权保护构成严重威胁。本文提出一种基于模型固有特征的简单而有效的鲁棒LLM指纹方法。我们发现，不同层间注意力参数矩阵的标准差分布呈现出独特且在大量持续训练后仍保持稳定的模式。这些参数分布特征可作为鲁棒指纹，可靠识别模型谱系并检测潜在版权侵权。跨多个模型家族的实验验证了该方法在模型认证中的有效性。值得注意的是，我们的研究发现华为近期发布的Pangu Pro MoE模型疑似通过“再利用”技术衍生自Qwen-2.5 14B模型，而非从零训练，揭示了潜在的模型抄袭、版权侵犯与信息伪造案例。这些发现强调了开发鲁棒指纹方法以保护大规模模型开发中知识产权的紧迫性，并指出仅靠刻意持续训练不足以完全掩盖模型来源。
