RedVLA: Physical Red Teaming for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Yuhao Zhang, Borong Zhang, Jiaming Fan, Jiachen Shen, Yishuai Cai, Yaodong Yang, Jiaming Ji
First: 2026-04-24T14:18:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:18:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The real-world deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains limited by the risk of unpredictable and irreversible physical harm. However, we currently lack effective mechanisms to proactively detect these physical safety risks before deployment. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{RedVLA}, the first red teaming framework for physical safety in VLA models. We systematically uncover unsafe behaviors through a two-stage process: (I) \textbf{Risk Scenario Synthesis} constructs a valid and task-feasible initial risk scene. Specifically, it identifies critical interaction regions from benign trajectories and positions the risk factor within these regions, aiming to entangle it with the VLA's execution flow and elicit a target unsafe behavior. (II) \textbf{Risk Amplification} ensures stable elicitation across heterogeneous models. It iteratively refines the risk factor state through gradient-free optimization guided by trajectory features. Experiments on six representative VLA models show that RedVLA uncovers diverse unsafe behaviors and achieves the ASR up to 95.5\% within 10 optimization iterations. To mitigate these risks, we further propose SimpleVLA-Guard, a lightweight safety guard built from RedVLA-generated data. Our data, assets, and code are available \href{https://redvla.github.io}{here}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RedVLA：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的物理红队测试框架
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在现实世界中的部署受限于其可能引发的不可预测且不可逆的物理危害风险。然而，目前尚缺乏有效机制在部署前主动检测此类物理安全风险。为填补这一空白，我们提出\textbf{RedVLA}，这是首个针对VLA模型物理安全的红队测试框架。我们通过两阶段流程系统性地揭示不安全行为：（I）\textbf{风险场景合成}构建一个有效且任务可行的初始风险场景。具体而言，它从良性轨迹中识别关键交互区域，并将风险因素置于这些区域，旨在将其与VLA的执行流程交织，从而引发目标不安全行为。（II）\textbf{风险放大}确保在异构模型中稳定触发风险。它通过基于轨迹特征的无梯度优化，迭代优化风险因素状态。在六个代表性VLA模型上的实验表明，RedVLA可揭示多样化的不安全行为，并在10次优化迭代内实现高达95.5\%的攻击成功率（ASR）。为缓解这些风险，我们进一步提出SimpleVLA-Guard，一种基于RedVLA生成数据构建的轻量级安全防护机制。我们的数据、资产和代码可在\href{https://redvla.github.io}{此处}获取。

It employs a two-stage method: first, Risk Scenario Synthesis constructs task-feasible initial risk scenes by embedding risk factors into critical interaction regions of benign trajectories to trigger unsafe actions; second, Risk Amplification refines these risk factors through gradient-free optimization guided by trajectory features to ensure consistent unsafe behavior across diverse models. Experiments on six VLA models demonstrate that RedVLA successfully elicits diverse unsafe behaviors with an attack success rate of up to 95.5% within just ten iterations, and it further enables the development of SimpleVLA-Guard, a lightweight safety guard trained on RedVLA-generated data.

FlowAnchor: Stabilizing the Editing Signal for Inversion-Free Video Editing
Authors: Ze Chen, Lan Chen, Yuanhang Li, Qi Mao
First: 2026-04-24T14:17:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:17:11+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We propose FlowAnchor, a training-free framework for stable and efficient inversion-free, flow-based video editing. Inversion-free editing methods have recently shown impressive efficiency and structure preservation in images by directly steering the sampling trajectory with an editing signal. However, extending this paradigm to videos remains challenging, often failing in multi-object scenes or with increased frame counts. We identify the root cause as the instability of the editing signal in high-dimensional video latent spaces, which arises from imprecise spatial localization and length-induced magnitude attenuation. To overcome this challenge, FlowAnchor explicitly anchors both where to edit and how strongly to edit. It introduces Spatial-aware Attention Refinement, which enforces consistent alignment between textual guidance and spatial regions, and Adaptive Magnitude Modulation, which adaptively preserves sufficient editing strength. Together, these mechanisms stabilize the editing signal and guide the flow-based evolution toward the desired target distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowAnchor achieves more faithful, temporally coherent, and computationally efficient video editing across challenging multi-object and fast-motion scenarios. The project page is available at https://cuc-mipg.github.io/FlowAnchor.github.io/.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of editing signals in high-dimensional video latent spaces that hinders inversion-free video editing, FlowAnchor introduces a training-free framework that stabilizes the editing process through spatial-aware attention refinement and adaptive magnitude modulation. These mechanisms ensure precise spatial localization and preserve editing strength across frames, enabling faithful, temporally coherent, and computationally efficient video editing even in complex multi-object and fast-motion scenarios. Experimental results show FlowAnchor outperforms existing methods in preserving structure and motion consistency under challenging conditions.
为了解决高维视频潜在空间中编辑信号不稳定导致的多目标或高速运动场景下编辑效果不佳的问题，FlowAnchor 提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过空间感知注意力细化对文本引导与空间区域进行对齐，以及自适应幅度调制保持编辑强度，从而稳定编辑过程。实验表明，该方法在复杂视频场景下能生成更忠实、时间连贯且计算高效的编辑结果，优于现有方法。
Adaptive Head Budgeting for Efficient Multi-Head Attention
Authors: Bilal Faye, Abdoulaye Mbaye, Hanane Azzag, Mustapha Lebbah
First: 2026-04-24T14:15:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:15:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers have become the dominant architecture across a wide range of domains, largely due to the effectiveness of multi-head attention in capturing diverse representation subspaces. However, standard multi-head attention activates all heads uniformly for every input, regardless of task requirements or input complexity. In many scenarios, particularly for coarse-grained tasks such as text classification, the relevant information is often global and does not require the full diversity of attention heads. As a consequence, using a fixed number of heads can introduce unnecessary computational cost or lead to suboptimal performance when the allocation does not match the input. To address this limitation, we introduce BudgetFormer, a Transformer architecture equipped with an adaptive multi-head attention mechanism that dynamically allocates computational resources. Our approach learns, for each input, both a head budget corresponding to the number of attention heads required, and a relevance distribution that selects the most informative heads. We also propose a training strategy based on an exploration and exploitation trade-off, allowing the model to discover effective head configurations before converging to efficient usage patterns. Experiments on text classification tasks of varying complexity show that our method reduces inference cost in terms of FLOPs and memory, while also achieving performance that can surpass standard full multi-head attention. These results highlight the potential of adaptive head allocation as a principled approach to improving both efficiency and effectiveness in Transformer models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向高效多头注意力的自适应头预算分配
Transformer因多头注意力在捕捉多样表征子空间方面的有效性，已成为众多领域的主流架构。然而，标准多头注意力对每个输入均匀激活所有头，无论任务需求或输入复杂度如何。在许多场景（如文本分类等粗粒度任务）中，相关信息往往是全局性的，无需全部注意力头的多样性。因此，固定数量的头可能导致不必要的计算开销或当资源分配不匹配输入时性能不佳。为解决此问题，我们提出BudgetFormer，一种配备自适应多头注意力机制的Transformer架构，可动态分配计算资源。我们的方法为每个输入学习所需头数的预算及选择最信息量头的分布。同时，我们提出一种基于探索与利用权衡的训练策略，使模型在收敛前发现有效头配置，进而实现高效使用。在不同复杂度的文本分类任务实验中，我们的方法在FLOPs和内存消耗上降低推理成本，同时性能可超越标准全多头注意力。结果表明，自适应头分配是提升Transformer模型效率与效果的有原则方法。

Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed multi-head attention in Transformer models, where all heads are uniformly activated regardless of input complexity, the paper proposes BudgetFormer, an adaptive attention mechanism that dynamically allocates computational resources per input. It learns a head budget (number of heads to activate) and a relevance distribution (which heads to prioritize) for each input, guided by an exploration-exploitation training strategy. Experimental results on text classification tasks demonstrate that BudgetFormer reduces inference FLOPs and memory usage while maintaining or even exceeding the performance of standard multi-head attention, validating its efficiency and effectiveness.
为解决Transformer中固定多头注意力在粗粒度任务中计算冗余的问题，本文提出BudgetFormer，一种动态分配计算资源的自适应注意力机制。该方法为每个输入学习所需的头数预算和相关性分布，以选择最有效的头，训练策略结合探索与利用。实验表明，该方法在文本分类任务中显著降低推理FLOPs和内存开销，同时性能可媲美甚至超越标准全多头注意力。
Explanation of Dynamic Physical Field Predictions using WassersteinGrad: Application to Autoregressive Weather Forecasting
Authors: Younes Essafouri, Laure Raynaud, Luciano Drozda, Laurent Risser
First: 2026-04-24T14:12:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:12:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As the demand to integrate Artificial Intelligence into high-stakes environments continues to grow, explaining the reasoning behind neural-network predictions has shifted from a theoretical curiosity to a strict operational requirement. Our work is motivated by the explanations of autoregressive neural predictions on dynamic physical fields, as in weather forecasting. Gradient-based feature attribution methods are widely used to explain the predictions on such data, in particular due to their scalability to high-dimensional inputs. It is also interesting to remark that gradient-based techniques such as SmoothGrad are now standard on images to robustify the explanations using pointwise averages of the attribution maps obtained from several noised inputs. Our goal is to efficiently adapt this aggregation strategy to dynamic physical fields. To do so, our first contribution is to identify a fundamental failure mode when averaging perturbed attribution maps on dynamic physical fields: stochastic input perturbations do not induce stationary amplitude noise in attribution maps, but instead cause a geometric displacement of the attributions. Consequently, pointwise averaging blurs these spatially misaligned features. To tackle this issue, we introduce WassersteinGrad, which extracts a geometric consensus of perturbed attribution maps by computing their entropic Wasserstein barycenter. The results, obtained on regional weather data and a meteorologist-validated neural model, demonstrate promising explainability properties of WassersteinGrad over gradient-based baselines across both single-step and autoregressive forecasting settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用WassersteinGrad解释动态物理场预测：应用于自回归天气预报
随着人工智能在高风险环境中的集成需求不断增长，解释神经网络预测背后的推理已从理论兴趣转变为严格的运营要求。我们的工作旨在解释动态物理场（如天气预报）中自回归神经网络预测的可解释性。梯度基特征归因方法因其对高维输入的良好可扩展性而广泛用于此类数据的解释。值得注意的是，梯度基技术（如SmoothGrad）如今已成为图像领域标准方法，通过多个噪声输入获得的归因图的逐点平均来增强解释的鲁棒性。我们的目标是高效地将这种聚合策略适配到动态物理场。为此，我们的第一项贡献是识别在动态物理场中对扰动归因图进行平均时的根本性失效模式：随机输入扰动不会在归因图中诱导稳定的幅值噪声，而是导致归因的空间位移。因此，逐点平均会模糊这些空间错位的特征。为解决此问题，我们引入WassersteinGrad，通过计算扰动归因图的熵性Wasserstein质心，提取其几何共识。实验结果基于区域天气数据和经气象学家验证的神经模型，表明WassersteinGrad在单步和自回归预测设置中均优于梯度基基线方法，展现出良好的可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to explain neural network predictions in high-stakes domains like weather forecasting, this work addresses the limitations of gradient-based attribution methods when applied to dynamic physical fields. It identifies that standard pointwise averaging of perturbed attribution maps fails because noise induces geometric shifts rather than amplitude noise, causing spatial misalignment and blurred explanations. To resolve this, the authors propose WassersteinGrad, which computes the entropic Wasserstein barycenter to extract a geometric consensus across perturbed maps. Experiments on regional weather data using a meteorologist-validated model show that WassersteinGrad provides clearer, more spatially coherent explanations than baseline methods in both single-step and autoregressive forecasting settings.
为满足高风险领域（如天气预报）中神经网络预测解释的需求，本文针对动态物理场中梯度归因方法的局限性提出解决方案：点对点平均扰动归因图会导致空间错位而非幅度噪声，从而模糊解释。为此，作者提出WassersteinGrad，通过计算扰动归因图的熵式Wasserstein重心提取几何共识。在区域气象数据上使用气象专家验证的模型实验表明，WassersteinGrad在单步和自回归预测场景下均优于梯度基线方法，具有更优的可解释性。
Useful nonrobust features are ubiquitous in biomedical images
Authors: Coenraad Mouton, Randle Rabe, Niklas C. Koser, Nicolai Krekiehn, Christopher Hansen, Jan-Bernd Hövener, Claus-C. Glüer
Venue: ISBI
First: 2026-04-24T14:12:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:12:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted at The IEEE International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study whether deep networks for medical imaging learn useful nonrobust features - predictive input patterns that are not human interpretable and highly susceptible to small adversarial perturbations - and how these features impact test performance. We show that models trained only on nonrobust features achieve well above chance accuracy across five MedMNIST classification tasks, confirming their predictive value in-distribution. Conversely, adversarially trained models that primarily rely on robust features sacrifice in-distribution accuracy but yield markedly better performance under controlled distribution shifts (MedMNIST-C). Overall, nonrobust features boost standard accuracy yet degrade out-of-distribution performance, revealing a practical robustness-accuracy trade-off in medical imaging classification tasks that should be tailored to the requirements of the deployment setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生物医学图像中普遍存在有用的非鲁棒特征
我们研究用于医学影像的深度网络是否学习到有用的非鲁棒特征——即不可被人理解但对微小对抗扰动高度敏感的预测性输入模式，以及这些特征如何影响测试性能。我们发现，仅在非鲁棒特征上训练的模型在五个MedMNIST分类任务中均取得远高于随机水平的准确率，证实了其在分布内预测价值。相反，对抗训练的模型主要依赖鲁棒特征，虽然牺牲了分布内准确率，但在受控分布偏移（MedMNIST-C）下表现显著更好。总体而言，非鲁棒特征提升了标准准确率，但降低了分布外性能，揭示了医学影像分类任务中实际存在的鲁棒性-准确率权衡，应根据部署场景需求进行调整。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how deep networks for medical imaging balance accuracy and robustness, this study investigates whether nonrobust features—predictive but human-uninterpretable patterns vulnerable to small perturbations—are prevalent and useful. The authors train models on nonrobust features alone and find they achieve significantly above-chance accuracy on five MedMNIST tasks, confirming their in-distribution predictive power. However, adversarially trained models that rely on robust features show reduced in-distribution accuracy but markedly improved performance under distribution shifts (MedMNIST-C). This reveals a trade-off: nonrobust features enhance standard accuracy but hurt out-of-distribution generalization, suggesting deployment settings should prioritize robustness when facing distributional shifts.
为理解医学影像深度学习模型如何在准确率与鲁棒性间权衡，本研究探讨了非鲁棒特征——即人类难以理解但对微小扰动敏感的预测性模式——是否普遍存在且有用。研究发现，仅使用非鲁棒特征训练的模型在五个MedMNIST任务上准确率显著高于随机水平，证实其在分布内预测能力。然而，对抗训练的模型虽牺牲了分布内准确率，但在分布偏移场景（MedMNIST-C）下表现明显更优，揭示了非鲁棒特征提升标准准确率但降低分布外鲁棒性的权衡关系，提示部署场景应根据实际需求调整模型设计。
"This Wasn't Made for Me": Recentering User Experience and Emotional Impact in the Evaluation of ASR Bias
Authors: Siyu Liang, Alicia Beckford Wassink
First: 2026-04-22T23:27:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:10:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Studies on bias in Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) tend to focus on reporting error rates for speakers of underrepresented dialects, yet less research examines the human side of system bias: how do system failures shape users' lived experiences, how do users feel about and react to them, and what emotional toll do these repeated failures exact? We conducted user experience studies across four U.S. locations (Atlanta, Gulf Coast, Miami Beach, and Tucson) representing distinct English dialect communities. Our findings reveal that most participants report technologies fail to consider their cultural backgrounds and require constant adjustment to achieve basic functionality. Despite these experiences, participants maintain high expectations for ASR performance and express strong willingness to contribute to model improvement. Qualitative analysis of open-ended narratives exposes the deeper costs of these failures. Participants report frustration, annoyance, and feelings of inadequacy, yet the emotional impact extends beyond momentary reactions. Participants recognize that systems were not designed for them, yet often internalize failures as personal inadequacy despite this critical awareness. They perform extensive invisible labor, including code-switching, hyper-articulation, and emotional management, to make failing systems functional. Meanwhile, their linguistic and cultural knowledge remains unrecognized by technologies that encode particular varieties as standard while rendering others marginal. These findings demonstrate that algorithmic fairness assessments based on accuracy metrics alone miss critical dimensions of harm: the emotional labor of managing repeated technological rejection, the cognitive burden of constant self-monitoring, and the psychological toll of feeling inadequate in one's native language variety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：‘这并非为我而设计’：重新审视ASR偏见评估中的用户体验与情感影响
关于自动语音识别（ASR）偏见的研究多聚焦于报告少数方言使用者的错误率，但较少探讨系统偏见对人类的影响：系统故障如何塑造用户的实际体验？用户对此有何感受与反应？这些反复失败带来何种情感代价？我们在美国四个代表不同英语方言社区的地区（亚特兰大、墨西哥湾沿岸、迈阿密海滩和图森）开展用户体验研究。研究发现，多数参与者表示技术未能考虑其文化背景，需不断调整才能实现基本功能。尽管如此，参与者仍对ASR性能抱有高期待，并强烈愿为模型改进贡献力量。对开放式叙述的质性分析揭示了这些失败的深层代价：参与者报告感到沮丧、恼怒和自我不足，但情感影响远不止瞬间反应。他们虽意识到系统并非为他们设计，却常将失败内化为个人能力不足。他们需付出大量隐形劳动——如语言转换、过度清晰发音和情绪管理——以使失效系统可用。同时，其语言与文化知识未被技术认可，技术将特定方言编码为标准，而将其他方言边缘化。这些发现表明，仅以准确率衡量的算法公平性评估忽略了关键伤害维度：管理反复技术拒绝所需的情感劳动、持续自我监控的认知负担，以及在母语方言中感到不足的心理代价。

This study addresses the gap in ASR bias research by shifting focus from technical error rates to the lived emotional and experiential impacts on users from underrepresented dialect communities. Through user experience studies in four U.S. locations, researchers documented how participants consistently encountered system failures that ignored their cultural and linguistic backgrounds, forcing them to engage in invisible labor like code-switching and hyper-articulation to compensate. Despite these challenges, participants maintained high expectations for ASR performance and expressed willingness to contribute to model improvement. Qualitative analysis revealed that repeated failures led to frustration, feelings of inadequacy, and emotional exhaustion, as users internalized system shortcomings as personal shortcomings, even while recognizing the systems were not designed for them.
该研究填补了ASR偏见研究中对技术错误率之外的人类体验与情感影响的关注空白。通过对美国四个代表不同英语方言社区的地区进行用户研究，研究人员发现使用者常遭遇技术忽视其文化背景，被迫进行如语言转换、过度清晰发音等隐形劳动以维持系统可用性。尽管面临这些困难，用户仍抱有高期望并乐于参与系统改进，但往往将失败归咎于自身不足，揭示了更深层的心理负担。研究结果表明，仅以准确率衡量的算法公平性评估忽略了用户所承受的情感与认知压力，包括持续的挫败感、自我监控负担以及母语被边缘化的心理创伤。
QuantClaw: Precision Where It Matters for OpenClaw
Authors: Manyi Zhang, Ji-Fu Li, Zhongao Sun, Xiaohao Liu, Zhenhua Dong, Xianzhi Yu, Haoli Bai, Xiaobo Xia
First: 2026-04-24T14:10:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:10:29+00:00
Comments: Blog: https://sparkengineai.github.io/QuantClaw
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous agent systems such as OpenClaw introduce significant efficiency challenges due to long-context inputs and multi-turn reasoning. This results in prohibitively high computational and monetary costs in real-world development. While quantization is a standard approach for reducing cost and latency, its impact on agent performance in realistic scenarios remains unclear. In this work, we analyze quantization sensitivity across diverse complex workflows over OpenClaw, and show that precision requirements are highly task-dependent. Based on this observation, we propose QuantClaw, a plug-and-play precision routing plugin that dynamically assigns precision according to task characteristics. QuantClaw routes lightweight tasks to lower-cost configurations while preserving higher precision for demanding workloads, saving cost and accelerating inference without increasing user complexity. Experiments show that our QuantClaw maintains or improves task performance while reducing both latency and computational cost. Across a range of agent tasks, it achieves up to 21.4% cost savings and 15.7% latency reduction on GLM-5 (FP8 baseline). These results highlight the benefit of treating precision as a dynamic resource in agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QuantClaw：为OpenClaw关键环节提供精准计算
自主代理系统如OpenClaw因长上下文输入和多轮推理带来显著效率挑战，导致实际开发中计算与成本过高。虽然量化是降低开销和延迟的标准方法，但其对代理系统在真实场景中的性能影响尚不明确。本文在OpenClaw上分析了不同复杂工作流的量化敏感性，发现精度需求高度依赖任务特性。基于此，我们提出QuantClaw，一种即插即用的精度路由插件，根据任务特征动态分配精度。QuantClaw将轻量任务路由至低成本配置，同时保留高精度处理复杂负载，实现成本节约与推理加速，且不增加用户复杂度。实验表明，QuantClaw在保持或提升任务性能的同时，降低延迟与计算成本。在多种代理任务中，其在GLM-5（FP8基线）上实现最高21.4%的成本节省和15.7%的延迟降低。结果凸显了在代理系统中将精度视为动态资源的价值。
Summary / 总结

Experiments demonstrate that QuantClaw maintains or improves performance while reducing latency and cost, achieving up to 21.4% cost savings and 15.7% latency reduction on GLM-5 with FP8 baseline.
为解决OpenClaw等自主代理系统因长上下文和多轮推理导致的高昂计算与经济成本，本研究分析了量化在不同复杂工作流中的敏感性，发现任务对精度的需求差异显著。基于此，提出QuantClaw插件，根据任务特性动态分配精度，轻量任务使用低精度配置，复杂任务保留高精度，从而降低成本并加速推理。实验表明，QuantClaw在保持或提升性能的同时，可实现最高21.4%的成本节省和15.7%的延迟降低（GLM-5 FP8基线）。
Building a Data Dashboard for Magic: The Gathering: Initial Design Considerations
Authors: Tomás Alves, João Moreira
First: 2025-12-10T16:15:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:09:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the initial stages of a design study to develop a dashboard for visualizing gameplay data in the Commander format of Magic: The Gathering. We conducted a user-task analysis to identify requirements for such a dashboard, followed by a design proposal addressing players' needs and common analysis tasks. We then carried out a structured user test to evaluate comprehension and preferences. Results show that players prioritize contextually relevant, outcome-driven metrics over peripheral ones, and that canonical charts (e.g., heatmaps, line charts) support higher comprehension than more complex visualizations like scatterplots or icicle plots. Our findings also highlight the importance of localized views, user customization, and progressive disclosure, emphasizing that adaptability and contextual relevance are as critical as accuracy in effective dashboard design. Overall, this study contributes practical guidelines for data visualization in gaming and broader insights for engagement-driven dashboards.

SpikingBrain2.0: Brain-Inspired Foundation Models for Efficient Long-Context and Cross-Platform Inference
Authors: Yuqi Pan, Jinghao Zhuang, Yupeng Feng, Fangzhi Zhong, Siyu Ding, Xuerui Qiu, Shaowei Gu, Bohan Sun, Zhiyong Qin, Yibo Zhong, Lingtao Ouyang, Kun Yang, Zehao Liu, Yuhong Chou, Shurong Wang, Anjie Hu, Han Xu, Bo Xu, Guoqi Li
First: 2026-04-24T14:07:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:07:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling context length is reshaping large-model development, yet full-attention Transformers suffer from prohibitive computation and inference bottlenecks at long sequences. A key challenge is to design foundation models that maintain performance and long-context efficiency with minimal training overhead. We introduce SpikingBrain2.0 (SpB2.0), a 5B model that advances both architecture and training efficiency of its predecessor. Our contributions are two-fold. (1) Architectural Innovation: We propose Dual-Space Sparse Attention (DSSA), an inter-layer hybrid of Sparse Softmax Attention (MoBA) and Sparse Linear Attention (SSE), achieving an improved performance-efficiency trade-off for long-context modeling. SpB2.0 further supports dual quantization paths: INT8-Spiking coding enables sparse event-driven computation, while FP8 coding accelerates inference on modern GPUs. (2) Enhanced Training Strategy: We develop an optimized Transformer-to-Hybrid (T2H) pipeline with dual conversion paths for LLMs and VLMs using curated open-source data. Empirically, SpB2.0-5B and SpB2.0-VL-5B recover most of the base Transformer (Qwen3-4B) capability with under 7k A100 GPU hours. SpB2.0 achieves a 10.13x TTFT speedup at 4M context and supports over 10M tokens on 8 A100 GPUs under vLLM, where full-attention models exceed memory limits. It also demonstrates strong cross-platform compatibility, enabling FP8 GPU inference (2.52x speedup at 250k) and efficient neuromorphic execution (64.31% sparsity, with 70.6% and 46.5% area and power reduction at 500MHz). Overall, SpikingBrain2.0 provides a practical pathway for lightweight, multimodal, spiking foundation models, highlighting the potential of combining brain-inspired mechanisms with efficient architectures for resource-constrained and edge scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpikingBrain2.0：面向高效长上下文与跨平台推理的脑启发基础模型
扩展上下文长度正在重塑大模型开发，但全注意力Transformer在长序列上面临计算和推理瓶颈。关键挑战在于设计基础模型，在保持性能与长上下文效率的同时，尽量减少训练开销。我们提出了SpikingBrain2.0（SpB2.0），一个5B规模的模型，其架构与训练效率均优于前代。 我们的贡献分为两点：（1）架构创新：我们提出双空间稀疏注意力（DSSA），是层间混合的稀疏Softmax注意力（MoBA）与稀疏线性注意力（SSE），实现了更优的长上下文建模性能-效率权衡。SpB2.0进一步支持双量化路径：INT8脉冲编码实现稀疏事件驱动计算，FP8编码加速现代GPU推理。（2）增强训练策略：我们开发了优化的Transformer到混合（T2H）流水线，针对LLM和VLM采用精选开源数据的双转换路径。 实证表明，SpB2.0-5B和SpB2.0-VL-5B在少于7000个A100 GPU小时下，恢复了大部分基础Transformer（Qwen3-4B）的能力。SpB2.0在4M上下文下TTFT加速10.13倍，且在8个A100 GPU上使用vLLM支持超1000万token，而全注意力模型因内存限制无法运行。它还展现出强跨平台兼容性，支持FP8 GPU推理（250k时加速2.52倍）和高效神经形态执行（64.31%稀疏度，500MHz下面积和功耗分别降低70.6%和46.5%）。 总体而言，SpikingBrain2.0为轻量级、多模态、脉冲基础模型提供了实用路径，凸显了结合脑启发机制与高效架构在资源受限和边缘场景中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of full-attention Transformers at long sequences, SpikingBrain2.0 introduces Dual-Space Sparse Attention (DSSA) to balance performance and efficiency, combining MoBA and SSE for sparse attention. It also supports dual quantization—INT8-Spiking for neuromorphic sparsity and FP8 for GPU acceleration. Trained via an optimized T2H pipeline on open-source data, the 5B model recovers most of Qwen3-4B’s capability using under 7k A100 GPU hours. Experimentally, it achieves 10.13x faster token throughput at 4M context, handles 10M tokens on 8 A100 GPUs, and shows strong cross-platform performance: 2.52x speedup on FP8 GPUs and 70.6% area reduction on neuromorphic chips with 64.31% sparsity.
为解决长序列下全注意力Transformer计算成本过高问题，SpikingBrain2.0提出双空间稀疏注意力（DSSA），融合MoBA与SSE实现性能与效率的平衡，并支持INT8脉冲编码（神经形态稀疏计算）与FP8（GPU加速）双量化路径。通过优化的T2H训练流水线，5B模型在少于7000个A100小时训练下复现了Qwen3-4B大部分能力。实验表明，其在4M上下文下实现10.13倍推理速度提升，8卡A100上支持1000万token推理，且在FP8 GPU上达2.52倍加速，在神经形态芯片上实现64.31%稀疏度、70.6%面积与46.5%功耗降低。
Adversarial Co-Evolution of Malware and Detection Models: A Bilevel Optimization Perspective
Authors: Olha Jurečková, Martin Jureček, Matouš Kozák, Róbert Lórencz
First: 2026-04-24T14:01:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T14:01:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning-based malware detectors are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial examples. Traditional defenses, such as one-shot adversarial training, often fail against adaptive attackers who use reinforcement learning to bypass detection. This paper proposes a robust defense framework based on bilevel optimization, explicitly modeling the strategic interaction between a defender and an attacker as an adversarial co-evolutionary process. We evaluate our approach using the MAB-malware framework against three distinct malware families: Mokes, Strab, and DCRat. Our experimental results demonstrate that while standard classifiers and basic adversarial retraining often remain vulnerable, showing evasion rates as high as 90 %, the proposed bilevel optimization approach consistently achieves near-total immunity, reducing evasion rates to 0 - 1.89 %. Furthermore, the iterative framework significantly increases the attacker's query complexity, raising the average cost of successful evasion by up to two orders of magnitude. These findings suggest that modeling the iterative cycle of attack and defense through bilevel optimization is essential for developing resilient malware detection systems capable of withstanding evolving adversarial threats.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of machine learning-based malware detectors to adaptive adversarial attacks, this paper introduces a bilevel optimization framework that models the strategic co-evolution between attackers and defenders. The method explicitly captures the iterative arms race by optimizing the detector’s defenses in response to the attacker’s evolving evasion strategies, using reinforcement learning to simulate adaptive adversaries. Experimental results on three malware families—Mokes, Strab, and DCRat—show that standard classifiers and basic adversarial retraining suffer evasion rates up to 90%, while the bilevel approach reduces evasion to 0–1.89%, and significantly increases the attacker’s query cost by up to two orders of magnitude.
为应对机器学习恶意软件检测器易受适应性对抗攻击的弱点，本文提出一种双层优化框架，将攻击者与防御者之间的战略共进化过程建模为对抗性博弈。该方法通过强化学习模拟适应性攻击者，动态优化检测器以应对攻击策略的演变。实验在Mokes、Strab和DCRat三个恶意软件家族上表明，传统分类器和基础对抗训练的逃逸率高达90%，而双层优化方法将逃逸率降至0–1.89%，并使攻击者成功逃逸的查询成本提升两个数量级。
Learning Evidence Highlighting for Frozen LLMs
Authors: Shaoang Li, Yanhang Shi, Yufei Li, Mingfu Liang, Xiaohan Wei, Yunchen Pu, Fei Tian, Chonglin Sun, Frank Shyu, Luke Simon, Sandeep Pandey, Xi Liu, Jian Li
First: 2026-04-24T13:57:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T13:57:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) can reason well, yet often miss decisive evidence when it is buried in long, noisy contexts. We introduce HiLight, an Evidence Emphasis framework that decouples evidence selection from reasoning for frozen LLM solvers. HiLight avoids compressing or rewriting the input, which can discard or distort evidence, by training a lightweight Emphasis Actor to insert minimal highlight tags around pivotal spans in the unaltered context. A frozen Solver then performs downstream reasoning on the emphasized input. We cast highlighting as a weakly supervised decision-making problem and optimize the Actor with reinforcement learning using only the Solver's task reward, requiring no evidence labels and no access to or modification of the Solver. Across sequential recommendation and long-context question answering, HiLight consistently improves performance over strong prompt-based and automated prompt-optimization baselines. The learned emphasis policy transfers zero-shot to both smaller and larger unseen Solver families, including an API-based Solver, suggesting that the Actor captures genuine, reusable evidence structure rather than overfitting to a single backbone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向冻结大语言模型的证据高亮学习
大语言模型（LLMs）虽推理能力强，但在长且嘈杂的上下文中常忽略关键证据。我们提出HiLight框架，将证据选择与推理解耦，适用于冻结的LLM求解器。HiLight避免压缩或重写输入（可能丢弃或扭曲证据），通过训练轻量级高亮Actor，在原始上下文中的关键片段周围插入最小化高亮标签。冻结的求解器随后在高亮输入上进行下游推理。我们将高亮视为弱监督决策问题，仅利用求解器的任务奖励，通过强化学习优化Actor，无需证据标签，也不需访问或修改求解器。在序列推荐与长上下文问答任务中，HiLight持续优于强提示基线及自动化提示优化基线。所学高亮策略可零样本迁移到未见的求解器家族（包括API求解器），表明Actor捕捉的是真实可复用的证据结构，而非对单一骨干模型过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that frozen large language models often overlook critical evidence buried in long, noisy contexts, HiLight introduces a lightweight reinforcement learning-based Emphasis Actor that inserts minimal highlight tags around key evidence spans without altering the original input. This decouples evidence selection from reasoning, allowing a frozen Solver to operate on the emphasized context. Evaluated across sequential recommendation and long-context QA tasks, HiLight consistently outperforms strong prompt-based and automated prompt-optimization baselines, and its learned emphasis policy transfers zero-shot to unseen Solver families, including API-based models, indicating it captures generalizable evidence structure rather than model-specific patterns.
为了解决冻结的大语言模型在长且嘈杂的上下文中常忽略关键证据的问题，HiLight提出一种轻量级强化学习框架，通过在原始输入中插入最小化高亮标签来突出关键证据片段，而不压缩或改写输入。该方法将证据选择与推理解耦，使冻结的求解器能在高亮后的内容上进行推理。在序列推荐和长上下文问答任务中，HiLight持续优于强提示基线和自动化提示优化方法，且其学习到的高亮策略可零样本迁移到未见过的求解器家族（包括API求解器），表明其捕捉的是可复用的真实证据结构而非特定模型的模式。
GOSPA and T-GOSPA quasi-metrics for evaluation of multi-object tracking algorithms
Authors: Ángel F. García-Fernández, Jinhao Gu, Lennart Svensson, Yuxuan Xia, Jan Krejčí, Oliver Kost, Ondřej Straka
Venue: IEEE Transactions on Aerospace and Electronic Systems, 2026
First: 2025-07-18T07:25:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T13:55:35+00:00
Comments: Matlab code of GOSPA and T-GOSPA q-metrics is provided at https://github.com/Agarciafernandez/MTT. Python code of the T-GOSPA q-metric is provided at https://github.com/Agarciafernandez/T-GOSPA-metric-python
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper introduces two quasi-metrics for performance assessment of multi-object tracking (MOT) algorithms. One quasi-metric is an extension of the generalised optimal subpattern assignment (GOSPA) metric and measures the discrepancy between sets of objects. The other quasi-metric is an extension of the trajectory GOSPA (T-GOSPA) metric and measures the discrepancy between sets of trajectories. Similar to the GOSPA-based metrics, these quasi-metrics include costs for localisation error for properly detected objects, the number of false objects and the number of missed objects. The T-GOSPA quasi-metric also includes a track switching cost. Differently from the GOSPA and T-GOSPA metrics, the proposed quasi-metrics have the flexibility of penalising missed and false objects with different costs, and the localisation costs are not required to be symmetric. We also explain how to obtain similarity score functions based on these quasi-metrics. The performance of several Bayesian MOT algorithms is assessed with the T-GOSPA quasi-metric via simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于多目标跟踪算法评估的GOSPA和T-GOSPA准度量
本文介绍了两种用于评估多目标跟踪（MOT）算法性能的准度量。一种准度量是广义最优子模式分配（GOSPA）度量的扩展，用于衡量目标集合间的差异；另一种是轨迹GOSPA（T-GOSPA）度量的扩展，用于衡量轨迹集合间的差异。与基于GOSPA的度量类似，这些准度量包含对正确检测目标的定位误差成本、假目标数量和漏检目标数量的成本。T-GOSPA准度量还包含轨迹切换成本。与GOSPA和T-GOSPA度量不同，所提出的准度量具有灵活惩罚漏检和假目标的不同成本的能力，且定位成本无需对称。我们还解释了如何基于这些准度量获得相似性评分函数。通过仿真，使用T-GOSPA准度量评估了几种贝叶斯MOT算法的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more flexible performance evaluation in multi-object tracking, this paper proposes two quasi-metrics—GOSPA and T-GOSPA extensions—that allow asymmetric penalties for localization errors and distinct costs for false and missed objects, unlike traditional metrics. These quasi-metrics measure discrepancies between object sets and trajectory sets, respectively, and include track switching costs in the trajectory version. Experimental results from simulations show that the proposed metrics effectively differentiate algorithm performance, particularly highlighting the impact of asymmetric penalties and track switching costs on evaluation outcomes.
为提升多目标跟踪算法评估的灵活性，本文提出两种准度量——GOSPA和T-GOSPA的扩展版本，允许对定位误差和漏检/虚检目标赋予非对称惩罚，而传统指标要求对称惩罚。这两种准度量分别衡量目标集合与轨迹集合的差异，并在轨迹版本中加入轨迹切换成本。仿真实验结果表明，所提指标能有效区分不同算法性能，尤其凸显非对称惩罚和轨迹切换成本对评估结果的影响。
Data-Free Contribution Estimation in Federated Learning using Gradient von Neumann Entropy
Authors: Asim Ukaye, Mubarak Abdu-Aguye, Nurbek Tastan, Karthik Nandakumar
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-24T13:55:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T13:55:10+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures, 4 pages Appendix, 6 figures in Appendix. To appear in CVPR 2026 FedVision Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Client contribution estimation in Federated Learning is necessary for identifying clients' importance and for providing fair rewards. Current methods often rely on server-side validation data or self-reported client information, which can compromise privacy or be susceptible to manipulation. We introduce a data-free signal based on the matrix von Neumann (spectral) entropy of the final-layer updates, which measures the diversity of the information contributed. We instantiate two practical schemes: (i) SpectralFed, which uses normalized entropy as aggregation weights, and (ii) SpectralFuse, which fuses entropy with class-specific alignment via a rank-adaptive Kalman filter for per-round stability. Across CIFAR-10/100 and the naturally partitioned FEMNIST and FedISIC benchmarks, entropy-derived scores show a consistently high correlation with standalone client accuracy under diverse non-IID regimes - without validation data or client metadata. We compare our results with data-free contribution estimation baselines and show that spectral entropy serves as a useful indicator of client contribution.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fairly assess client contributions in federated learning without compromising privacy or relying on unreliable client reports, this work proposes a data-free method using gradient von Neumann entropy to quantify information diversity in client updates. Two schemes, SpectralFed and SpectralFuse, are introduced: the former assigns weights based on normalized entropy, while the latter integrates entropy with class alignment via a rank-adaptive Kalman filter for stability. Experiments across CIFAR-10/100 and FEMNIST/FedISIC benchmarks demonstrate that entropy scores consistently correlate with client accuracy across non-IID data distributions, outperforming existing data-free baselines and validating entropy as a reliable proxy for contribution.
为在联邦学习中无需依赖隐私泄露或易被操纵的客户端数据，公平评估客户端贡献，本文提出基于梯度冯·诺依曼熵的无数据方法，量化客户端更新的信息多样性。提出两种实用方案：SpectralFed 依据归一化熵分配权重，SpectralFuse 则结合熵与类别对齐，通过秩自适应卡尔曼滤波提升每轮稳定性。在 CIFAR-10/100 和 FEMNIST/FedISIC 等非独立同分布数据集上，熵值得分与客户端准确率高度相关，优于现有无数据基线方法，验证了熵作为贡献度指标的有效性。
Cross-Stage Coherence in Hierarchical Driving VQA: Explicit Baselines and Learned Gated Context Projectors
Authors: Gautam Kumar Jain, Carsten Markgraf, Julian Stähler
First: 2026-04-24T13:54:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T13:54:48+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Visual Question Answering (GVQA) for autonomous driving organizes reasoning into ordered stages, namely Perception, Prediction, and Planning, where planning decisions should remain consistent with the model's own perception. We present a comparative study of cross-stage context passing on DriveLM-nuScenes using two complementary mechanisms. The explicit variant evaluates three prompt-based conditioning strategies on a domain-adapted 4B VLM (Mini-InternVL2-4B-DA-DriveLM) without additional training, reducing NLI contradiction by up to 42.6% and establishing a strong zero-training baseline. The implicit variant introduces gated context projectors, which extract a hidden-state vector from one stage and inject a normalized, gated projection into the next stage's input embeddings. These projectors are jointly trained with stage-specific QLoRA adapters on a general-purpose 8B VLM (InternVL3-8B-Instruct) while updating only approximately 0.5% of parameters. The implicit variant achieves a statistically significant 34% reduction in planning-stage NLI contradiction (bootstrap 95% CIs, p < 0.05) and increases cross-stage entailment by 50%, evaluated with a multilingual NLI classifier to account for mixed-language outputs. Planning language quality also improves (CIDEr +30.3%), but lexical overlap and structural consistency degrade due to the absence of driving-domain pretraining. Since the two variants use different base models, we present them as complementary case studies: explicit context passing provides a strong training-free baseline for surface consistency, while implicit gated projection delivers significant planning-stage semantic gains, suggesting domain adaptation as a plausible next ingredient for full-spectrum improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分层驾驶视觉问答中的跨阶段一致性：显式基线与学习门控上下文投影器
自动驾驶领域的图视觉问答（GVQA）将推理划分为有序阶段：感知、预测与规划，其中规划决策应与模型自身的感知保持一致。我们在DriveLM-nuScenes上比较了两种互补的跨阶段上下文传递机制。显式方法在无额外训练的情况下，对适配领域的4B视觉语言模型（Mini-InternVL2-4B-DA-DriveLM）评估了三种基于提示的条件化策略，将NLI矛盾降低最多达42.6%，建立了强大的零训练基线。隐式方法引入门控上下文投影器，从当前阶段提取隐藏状态向量，并将其归一化、门控投影注入下一阶段的输入嵌入。这些投影器与针对各阶段的QLoRA适配器联合训练于通用8B视觉语言模型（InternVL3-8B-Instruct），仅更新约0.5%的参数。隐式方法在规划阶段NLI矛盾上实现统计显著性降低34%（Bootstrap 95%置信区间，p < 0.05），跨阶段蕴含度提升50%，评估采用多语言NLI分类器以应对混合语言输出。规划语言质量亦提升（CIDEr +30.3%），但因缺乏驾驶领域预训练，词汇重叠与结构一致性下降。由于两种方法使用不同基模型，我们将其作为互补案例研究：显式上下文传递提供表面一致性方面的强零训练基线，而隐式门控投影则带来规划阶段显著的语义增益，暗示领域适配可能是实现全面改进的合理下一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for consistent reasoning across stages in hierarchical driving VQA, this study compares two context-passing mechanisms on DriveLM-nuScenes. The explicit method uses prompt-based conditioning on a domain-adapted 4B VLM without additional training, reducing NLI contradictions by up to 42.6%. The implicit method employs gated context projectors that extract and inject normalized hidden-state vectors between stages, jointly trained with QLoRA adapters on an 8B VLM while updating only 0.5% of parameters. The implicit variant significantly reduces planning-stage NLI contradictions by 34% (p < 0.05) and boosts cross-stage entailment by 50%, while also improving planning language quality (CIDEr +30.3%), though at the cost of reduced lexical and structural consistency due to lack of domain pretraining.
为提升自动驾驶场景中多阶段视觉问答的推理一致性，该研究对比了两种上下文传递机制。显式方法在适配的4B视觉语言模型上通过提示条件化实现零训练，将NLI矛盾率降低最高42.6%；隐式方法引入门控上下文投影器，在8B模型上联合QLoRA适配器训练，仅更新0.5%参数，显著降低规划阶段NLI矛盾34%（p<0.05），提升跨阶段蕴含关系50%，同时规划语言质量（CIDEr）提升30.3%，但因缺乏领域预训练，词表重叠与结构一致性下降。
SOLAR-RL: Semi-Online Long-horizon Assignment Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Jichao Wang, Liuyang Bian, Yufeng Zhou, Han Xiao, Yue Pan, Guozhi Wang, Hao Wang, Zhaoxiong Wang, Yafei Wen, Xiaoxin Chen, Shuai Ren, Lingfang Zeng
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-24T13:53:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-24T13:53:39+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 11 figures. Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) mature, GUI agents are evolving from static interactions to complex navigation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training MLLM agents on dynamic GUI tasks, its effective application faces a dilemma. Standard Offline RL often relies on static step-level data, neglecting global trajectory semantics such as task completion and execution quality. Conversely, Online RL captures the long-term dynamics but suffers from high interaction costs and potential environmental instability. To bridge this gap, we propose SOLAR-RL (Semi-Online Long-horizon Assignment Reinforcement Learning). Instead of relying solely on expensive online interactions, our framework integrates global trajectory insights directly into the offline learning process. Specifically, we reconstruct diverse rollout candidates from static data, detect the first failure point using per-step validity signals, and retroactively assign dense step-level rewards with target-aligned shaping to reflect trajectory-level execution quality, effectively simulating online feedback without interaction costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SOLAR-RL significantly improves long-horizon task completion rates and robustness compared to strong baselines, offering a sample-efficient solution for autonomous GUI navigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOLAR-RL：半在线长时程任务分配强化学习
随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的成熟，GUI代理正从静态交互演进为复杂导航。尽管强化学习（RL）已成为训练MLLM代理处理动态GUI任务的有前景范式，但其有效应用面临困境：标准离线RL依赖静态步级数据，忽略任务完成与执行质量等全局轨迹语义；而在线RL虽能捕捉长期动态，却面临高交互成本与环境不稳定风险。为弥合此差距，我们提出SOLAR-RL（半在线长时程任务分配强化学习）。该框架无需依赖昂贵的在线交互，直接将全局轨迹洞察整合进离线学习过程：具体而言，我们从静态数据重构多样化的 rollout 候选轨迹，利用每步有效性信号检测首次失败点，并回溯性地分配与目标对齐的密集步级奖励，以反映轨迹级执行质量，从而在无交互成本下模拟在线反馈。大量实验表明，SOLAR-RL相比强基线显著提升长时程任务完成率与鲁棒性，为自主GUI导航提供样本高效的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient training of GUI agents using MLLMs, SOLAR-RL addresses the trade-off between offline RL’s static data limitations and online RL’s high cost. It introduces a semi-online framework that leverages offline data to simulate online feedback by reconstructing diverse rollouts, identifying failure points via validity signals, and retroactively assigning dense rewards aligned with trajectory-level task success. Experiments show SOLAR-RL achieves significantly higher long-horizon task completion rates and robustness compared to baselines, demonstrating sample efficiency without requiring live interactions.
为提升多模态大语言模型在GUI导航任务中的训练效率，SOLAR-RL提出一种半在线框架，通过离线数据重构多样化轨迹、利用有效性信号定位首次失败点，并反向分配与任务目标对齐的密集奖励，模拟在线反馈而无需真实交互。实验表明，该方法在长时任务完成率和鲁棒性上显著优于现有基线，实现高效样本利用。
